Ads Platform / neo Ads 面试准备#

总体理解与面试定位#

1. 这类 Ads Platform 岗位的核心本质是什么？#

Ads Platform 不是简单地“在视频里插广告”，而是一个同时服务 members、advertisers、publishers/supply partners、sales/ad ops、finance 和 measurement 的复杂交易系统。它要在毫秒级 decisioning、全球 streaming 体验、品牌安全、预算节奏、频控、定向、报告、计费和隐私之间做平衡。neo Ads 的特殊点是 CTV/streaming-first、会员体验极重要、内容 metadata 很深、live events 如 NFL/WWE 带来超高并发和实时 DAI 挑战，同时还在建设 proprietary neo Ads Suite 和 GenAI-powered creative/ad formats。

2. 面试中如何概括自己的匹配度？#

可以这样说：我把 neo Ads 理解成 high-scale distributed systems + real-time decisioning + ML optimization + privacy-safe data platform + premium streaming UX 的结合。这个系统既要像 ad exchange 一样实时和高吞吐，又要像 neo playback 一样可靠和体验优先。我适合的点是能在 backend infra、ML serving、eventing/data quality、platform foundations、privacy/security、product workflow 之间建清楚边界，让广告业务增长不会牺牲 member experience 和 operational correctness。

3. neo Ads 和传统 web/mobile ads 有什么不同？#

传统 web/mobile ads 常是页面/Feed/短视频场景，广告请求粒度更碎，RTB 生态成熟；neo Ads 是 premium CTV/streaming 体验，ad break、pod、content context、device playback、live manifest、brand safety、frequency capping、creative quality 都更关键。用户容忍度更低：一次 ad insertion 错误可能直接破坏观看体验。广告不是旁边一个 banner，而是嵌入内容播放链路，所以 serving infra 必须和 streaming playback、manifest、client SDK 深度集成。

4. 你应该反复强调的系统原则#

Ads is a marketplace under user-experience constraints. 系统既要 maximize revenue 和 advertiser outcomes，又要 protect member experience。每一次 ad decision 都是多目标优化：relevance、pacing、budget、frequency cap、policy、brand safety、latency、fairness、measurement、billing correctness。面试中要避免只讲 revenue maximization，要一直带上 viewer experience 和 trust。

5. 高层业务地图怎么讲？#

可以把 neo Ads 拆成五层：第一，Supply，包括 neo inventory、external TV publishers、live events、ad formats；第二，Demand，包括 direct-sold campaigns、programmatic demand、advertisers/agencies/DSPs；第三，Decisioning，包括 targeting、ranking、auction、budget/pacing、frequency cap、policy；第四，Delivery & Experience，包括 client/server-side ad insertion、DAI、podding、creative serving；第五，Data & Business Ops，包括 eventing、measurement、reporting、billing、forecasting、pricing、planning、sales workflow。

neo Ads 组织/团队地图#

1. Ad Server Platform 做什么？#

Ad Server Platform 是 ads backend 的基础层，负责 scalable/resilient frameworks for ad decisioning、targeting、frequency capping、policy enforcement。它还要支撑 live events 的 real-time dynamic ad insertion。面试里可以理解为：这是毫秒级广告请求路径的核心基础设施，要求 low latency、high availability、global scale、correctness 和 clear policy boundaries。

2. Ad Decisioning & Optimization 做什么？#

这个团队负责实时广告选择：ranking/scoring、ML model serving、auction mechanics、budget/pacing、goal-based delivery、podding、traffic shaping。它的目标不是简单选最高价广告，而是在 revenue、advertiser outcome、campaign constraints、member experience 之间动态优化。系统设计会问 ranking service、pacing system、auction、frequency cap、model serving、online/offline feedback loop。

3. Ads Member Experience 做什么？#

它连接 neo clients（TV、mobile、web）和 ads serving infra，负责不同 ad formats 如何融入会员体验。重点是 playback UX、format integration、client/server contract、ad break rendering、skip/interactive/modular formats、error recovery。面试中要强调：client integration 不是 UI 小事，它决定广告请求时机、latency budget、fallback、measurement fidelity。

4. Ads Programmatic 做什么？#

Programmatic 团队构建 third-party demand integrations，让 DSP/advertising partners 通过 programmatic channels 购买 neo inventory。它涉及 OpenRTB-like integrations、bid request/response、deal IDs、brand safety、latency、demand quality、partner reliability、revenue optimization。neo 的挑战是把外部 ad tech 生态接入 premium streaming experience，同时保持控制和质量。

5. Ad Supply 做什么？#

Ad Supply 是 external supply integration，把 third-party TV publishers/broadcasters 接入 neo streaming platform，成为可 monetizable ad inventory。核心是复杂 inventory split、pre-negotiated conditions、publisher demand vs neo demand、content rights、ad rules 和 reporting/reconciliation。它桥接传统 TV distribution 和 streaming ad ecosystem。

6. Ads Eventing 做什么？#

Ads Eventing 是 foundational data plane，收集、验证、 enrich、deliver ads events。来源包括 client devices、server-side ad insertion、third-party integrations。下游包括 ad serving decisions、measurement、attribution、billing、financial reconciliation。这个团队的关键词是 high-throughput pipelines、data quality、privacy-safe streams、exactness、latency 和 schema governance。

7. Ads Identity 做什么？#

Ads Identity 构建 Ads Identity Graph 和 Identity Resolution services。目标是提高 identifier coverage、支持 advertiser/partner data matching，同时保护 member privacy。它涉及 deterministic/probabilistic matching、household/device/account identity、privacy controls、clean room、consent、data minimization。面试中要强调：identity 是 ads effectiveness 的基础，也是 privacy risk 的核心。

8. Signals & Targeting / Audience Activation 做什么？#

Signals & Targeting 负责 audience/contextual targeting、supply eligibility、outcome-based optimization、probabilistic audiences。Audience Activation 负责 ingest advertiser custom audiences、conversion data、retargeting audiences。它们共同让系统知道谁 eligible、谁 relevant、哪些 signals 可用于 decisioning 和 measurement。核心挑战是 freshness、privacy、match rate、latency、accuracy、policy。

9. Data Clean Room 做什么？#

Clean Room 提供 privacy-safe data collaboration，允许 neo 和 external partners 共享/计算受控数据，而不是直接暴露 raw user-level data。它支持 measurement、activation、audience overlap、conversion analysis 等场景。面试里要讲 privacy boundary、aggregation threshold、access control、query policy、audit、differential privacy/clean room semantics。

10. Platform Foundations 做什么？#

Platform Foundations 提供 Ads organization 的 shared services：UI infrastructure、common components、access control、Public API、GenAI foundations、Ads Entity Service/knowledge graph。它提升开发速度和一致性。面试重点是 platform thinking：common abstractions、entity model、authz、developer velocity、governance、reusability。

11. Creatives Infrastructure / Creative Studio 做什么？#

Creative Infrastructure 支撑 creative workflows：process、review、deploy advertiser creatives 到正确成员，支持更复杂 ad units 和 device variety，并利用 ML/GenAI。Creative Studio 管理 advertiser-facing creative tools、building experience、preview/approval、GenAI features。重点是 creative lifecycle、asset validation、brand safety、approval workflow、format compatibility、GenAI creative generation。

12. Forecasting & Simulation 做什么？#

它构建 near-realtime forecasting services 和 offline simulation systems，支持 inventory decisioning、media planning、yield optimization、experimentation。核心是预测未来 inventory、campaign delivery、reach/frequency、availability 和 scenario outcome。系统横跨 high-scale distributed systems、ML infra、data-intensive computation。

13. Pricing & Packaging / Media Planning / Campaign Management 做什么？#

Pricing & Packaging 管 rate card、dynamic reserve prices、inventory split、yield optimization、ad product catalog、centralized pricing engine。Media Planning 把 advertiser objectives 转成 media plans。Campaign Management 管 OMS、orders、line items、booking/execution。它们是广告商业流程的中台：从销售承诺到系统投放，再到监控和履约。

14. Live Ads Management 做什么？#

Live Ads Management 负责 live events 的广告计划、设置和投放：automated manifest generation、ML optimization、agentic workflows、real-time decisioning。Live 最大挑战是并发、低延迟、不可重来、break timing 动态、ad pod 约束和 viewer experience。它是 neo Ads 里最有技术挑战和曝光度的方向之一。

15. Reporting / Measurement / Finance 做什么？#

Reporting 提供 advertiser-facing APIs/UI，展示准确、及时、可切片的 campaign performance。Measurement 量化 campaign outcomes，用 first/third-party 方法做 attribution。Finance 做 compliant revenue recognition、billing、invoicing、journal automation。它们依赖 Ads Eventing 的数据质量；如果事件错，reporting、measurement、billing 都会错。

Ads Core Concepts 高频基础#

1. 什么是 inventory？#

Inventory 是可售卖的广告机会，例如某内容、某设备、某地区、某时间、某 ad break/pod 中的 slot。CTV/streaming inventory 还带有 content metadata、ad load、break position、live/VOD、device、member context、policy constraints。Inventory 是 forecasting、pricing、planning、decisioning 的共同语言。

2. 什么是 campaign / order / line item？#

Order 通常代表 advertiser/agreement 层级，line item 定义具体投放目标、budget、flight dates、targeting、frequency cap、creative、goal。Campaign Management/OMS 管这些对象。Ad server 在每次请求中判断哪些 line items eligible，再 ranking/auction/optimization。面试中要强调 hierarchy 和 lifecycle：draft、booked、active、paused、completed、billed。

3. 什么是 ad decisioning？#

Ad decisioning 是对一次 ad opportunity 选择哪个 ad 或 ad pod。输入包括 member/context、content metadata、eligible campaigns、targeting rules、frequency caps、budget/pacing、creative constraints、policy、auction bids、latency budget。输出包括 selected ad(s)、tracking metadata、price/reason、fallback。它是 ads 系统的实时大脑。

4. 什么是 frequency capping？#

Frequency capping 控制用户在一定时间内看到同一个 advertiser/campaign/creative 的次数，保护 member experience，也避免浪费预算。挑战是跨设备、跨 session、server/client events 延迟、identity resolution、实时读写。设计时要考虑 strong vs eventual consistency：低延迟 ad serving 可能不能每次全局强一致，但要避免明显过曝。

5. 什么是 pacing？#

Pacing 控制 campaign budget 或 impression goal 在 flight period 内合理消耗，避免过早花完或投不出去。简单做法是按时间线性分配，复杂做法会考虑 forecasted supply、auction win rate、performance、seasonality、live events。Pacing 是 optimization 系统核心：既要满足 advertiser delivery，又要最大化 yield 和 member experience。

6. 什么是 podding？#

Podding 是在一个 ad break 中组织多个 ads。需要考虑 pod length、ad duration、competitive separation、frequency caps、creative diversity、position、brand safety、campaign priority。CTV 广告体验很像电视 ad break，podding 做不好会出现重复广告、同类竞争广告挨着、时长超限、体验差。Live DAI 中 podding 和 manifest timing 紧密相关。

7. 什么是 DAI？#

Dynamic Ad Insertion 是在视频流中动态插入广告。VOD 可以提前或播放时插入；live events 需要实时根据 ad breaks 和 manifest 更新插入。DAI 要协调 ad decisioning、creative availability、transcoding、manifest manipulation、tracking events、client playback。它比普通 display ads 难很多，因为一旦 playback 出错，用户直接感知。

8. 什么是 brand safety？#

Brand safety 保证广告不会出现在不合适的内容、上下文或用户体验中，也保证 creative 本身符合政策。neo 有深 content metadata，可用于 contextual targeting 和 exclusion。Brand safety 包括内容分级、主题、地理、年龄、敏感类别、advertiser conflicts、policy enforcement。它保护 advertiser trust，也保护 member experience。

9. 什么是 measurement / attribution？#

Measurement 量化广告是否带来 brand lift、conversion、reach、frequency、incrementality 等结果。Attribution 把用户行为或转化归因到曝光。挑战是 privacy、identity matching、cross-device、delayed conversions、third-party data、clean rooms、统计偏差。好的 measurement 系统必须准确、可解释、可审计。

10. 什么是 reconciliation / billing？#

Reconciliation 比对 ad delivery events、pricing terms、campaign contracts、third-party reports，形成计费和财务确认。广告系统里 event correctness 直接影响收入确认。Finance 平台需要 compliant revenue recognition、invoice、journal、credit、pricing validation。面试里这体现 ads 不是只实时 serving，还有强 business correctness。

Ad Server / Decisioning 系统设计#

这一 tab 要按普通 system design 面试的方式来讲：先给大图，再落到组件、数据模型、请求路径、状态一致性、ML ranking、fallback、metrics。不要只说 “ad server selects ads”；面试官会追问 Kafka、Redis、S3、CDN、DSP timeout、pacing state、frequency cap consistency、manifest latency。

0. Serving / Streaming / Programmatic 总架构图#

text
Client / Playback / Manifest Request
  -> CDN / Edge / API Gateway
  -> Ad Request Gateway
  -> Context Builder
       -> Identity Service
       -> Privacy / Consent Service
       -> Content Metadata Service
       -> Device / Geo / Session Context
  -> Candidate Retrieval
       -> Campaign Serving Index
          (OpenSearch / Elasticsearch / in-memory index)
  -> Eligibility Filters
       -> Targeting Engine
       -> Frequency Cap Store
          (Redis Cluster / DynamoDB / Cassandra)
       -> Pacing State Cache
          (Redis / DynamoDB)
       -> Policy Engine
       -> Creative Readiness Cache
  -> Demand Adapters
       -> Direct-sold demand
       -> Programmatic Partner Gateway
          -> DSP / SSP partners with timeout + circuit breaker
  -> ML Scoring / Auction / Podding
       -> Feature Store online
          (Redis / Cassandra / DynamoDB)
       -> Model Serving
          (Triton / Ray Serve / KServe)
  -> Creative Resolver
       -> S3 / object store
       -> CDN URLs
  -> Ad Decision Response
  -> Decision / Impression Events
       -> Kafka / Kinesis / Pulsar
       -> Flink / Spark Streaming
       -> S3 / Iceberg + OLAP + Reporting

面试里可以先说：

I split the system into a low-latency serving path and an asynchronous data/control path. The serving path reads precomputed indexes, Redis/KV state and model scores; it does not scan warehouses. The data path uses Kafka/Flink/S3/Iceberg/OLAP to update counters, reports, models and billing.

0A. Component Stack：普通系统设计必须说清楚#

LayerComponentsWhy
edge/requestCDN, WAF, API Gateway, Envoy/ALBabsorb traffic, route by region, protect origin
service runtimeKubernetes/ECS, gRPC/REST, service meshautoscaling, deploy, mTLS, retries
candidate indexOpenSearch/Elasticsearch/custom in-memory indexfilter campaigns by geo/device/content/flight/format
state cacheRedis Cluster, Memcachedlow-latency cap, pacing, features, short-lived session state
durable KVDynamoDB, Cassandra, Bigtablehigh-QPS counters and serving metadata
event streamKafka, Kinesis, Pulsardecisions, impressions, clicks, conversions, partner callbacks
stream computeFlink, Spark Structured Streaming, Kafka Streamsdedup, enrichment, counters, freshness
object storeS3, GCS, Azure Blobcreative assets, manifests, logs, offline snapshots
media/CDNCDN, origin, transcoder, DRM/packagercreative delivery and streaming integration
ML servingTriton, Ray Serve, KServe, SageMaker Endpointranking, calibration, quality models
feature storeRedis online + Iceberg/S3 offline, Feasttraining/serving consistency
observabilityOpenTelemetry, Prometheus, Grafana, Datadogtrace ad request, model, partner, manifest, event lag
policy/secretsOPA, IAM, KMS, Vaultprivacy, partner credentials, policy-as-code

1. 设计一个 ad decisioning service#

1.1 Requirements#

在线请求 p95/p99 latency 可控，不能阻塞 playback。 · 支持 direct-sold、programmatic、house ads、sponsorship。 · 支持 targeting、frequency cap、pacing、policy、creative readiness。 · 支持 ML ranking / auction / podding。 · 支持 traceability：为什么选中、为什么过滤、用了哪个 index/model/config。 · 支持 graceful fallback：模型、DSP、cap store、creative resolver 都可能失败。

1.2 Request path#

text
POST /adDecision
  -> validate request
  -> build context
  -> retrieve candidates
  -> apply hard filters
  -> call cap/pacing/policy stores
  -> score candidates
  -> run auction / priority rules
  -> generate pod
  -> resolve creative URLs
  -> return decision + tracking metadata
  -> async write decision log to Kafka

1.3 Core API#

text
Request:
  request_id
  member/profile/household/device identifiers
  privacy_flags / consent
  content_id / genre / rating / live_event_id
  ad_break_id / pod_duration / format
  geo / locale / device_type / app_version

Response:
  decision_id
  pod:
    creative_id
    ad_id
    duration_ms
    media_url
    tracking_urls
  decision_reasons
  fallback_reason
  model_version
  index_version

1.4 Concrete components#

Ad Request Gateway：rate limit、auth、request validation、routing。 · Context Builder：调用 identity/privacy/content/device/supply services。 · Campaign Serving Index：OpenSearch/Elasticsearch 或 custom in-memory index。 · Frequency Cap Store：Redis Cluster + durable correction store。 · Pacing State Cache：Redis/DynamoDB 中的 throttle/priority/bid multiplier。 · Model Serving：Triton/Ray Serve/KServe，支持 timeout fallback。 · Creative Resolver：只返回 approved + CDN ready creative。 · Event Producer：decision/impression candidate logs 写 Kafka。

1.5 Data model#

text
Campaign:
  campaign_id, advertiser_id, objective, flight, budget, status

LineItem:
  line_item_id, campaign_id, priority, targeting_spec, pacing_policy, bid/floor

Creative:
  creative_id, duration, approval_status, cdn_status, device_compatibility

DecisionLog:
  request_id, decision_id, selected_ads, rejected_reasons,
  model_version, feature_version, index_version, latency_breakdown

1.6 Senior trade-offs#

强一致 budget/cap vs 低延迟：大多数请求用 regional cache + async correction，少数严格 cap 使用 stronger path。 · ML optimality vs playback：模型超时必须 fallback，不能卡住播放。 · personalization vs privacy：privacy flags 决定能用哪些 identity/user features。 · direct-sold delivery vs programmatic yield：用 priority/pacing/yield optimizer 共同决策。

1.7 Metrics#

p50/p95/p99 decision latency。 · fill rate、eligible candidates/request。 · fallback rate by dependency。 · cap overrun、budget overdelivery。 · model timeout rate、feature freshness。 · decision reason coverage。 · ad playback error and repeated ad rate。

2. Ad decisioning 的 latency budget 怎么拆？#

Latency budget 要拆到组件级：

StepComponentTypical strategy
request validationgateway/servicelocal CPU, strict schema
identity/privacy lookupidentity service/cacheRedis/local cache, timeout to contextual
candidate retrievalOpenSearch/in-memory indexprecomputed serving index
cap/pacing lookupRedis/DynamoDB/Cassandrabatch get, local fallback
policy/targetingpolicy enginecompiled rules / policy-as-code
feature fetchonline feature storeRedis/Cassandra, feature freshness check
model scoringTriton/Ray Servebatch scoring + timeout fallback
auction/poddingservice memoryheuristic optimization
creative resolvereadiness cache + CDN metadatano large object read in request path
loggingKafka producerasync, non-blocking

回答模板：

I would put strict timeouts around every dependency. If identity lookup fails, fall back to contextual targeting. If model scoring times out, use cached score or rule-based ranking. If DSPs are slow, exclude them. Playback SLO is mandatory; external demand and model optimality are opportunistic.

3. Candidate retrieval 怎么做？#

Candidate retrieval 的目标是快速缩小候选集，不是做最终排序。

text
Campaign Management / OMS
  -> validation
  -> campaign metadata DB
  -> index builder
  -> regional serving index
       (OpenSearch / Elasticsearch / custom in-memory index)
  -> ad server candidate retrieval

Index fields：

geo / region。 · device / platform。 · content category / rating / live/VOD。 · ad format / duration。 · flight start/end。 · deal id / priority tier。 · coarse targeting keys。 · creative readiness。

Senior nuance：

粗过滤放 index，精细 eligibility 放 service。 · index version 要写进 decision log。 · campaign 更新需要 near-real-time publish，但必须支持 rollback。 · generated/invalid/expired line item 不应进入 serving index。 · hot campaign 可以缓存 retrieval result，但 cache key 要包含 privacy/context scope。

Metrics：

retrieval latency。 · candidates/request。 · empty candidate rate。 · stale index rate。 · rejected reason distribution。 · index publish lag。

4. Frequency cap 如何在分布式系统中实现？#

这题不要只说 Redis。要讲 read path、write path、event correction。

text
Ad Decision
  -> batch read cap counters
       Redis Cluster / DynamoDB / Cassandra
  -> filter over-cap candidates
  -> provisional exposure write
  -> selected ad response

Validated Impression Events
  -> Kafka
  -> Flink dedup + late event handling
  -> cap correction update

Data model：

text
cap_key = identity_scope + entity_scope + entity_id + window
entity_scope = creative | campaign | advertiser | category
value = count, last_updated_at, source, confidence, ttl

Trade-off：

global strong consistency 会伤 latency。 · regional/local counters 快，但 failover/cross-device 会有重复风险。 · validated impression correction 可以修正 provisional selected-ad 写入。

Debug 重复广告：

identity resolution 是否换 key。 · impression event 是否 lag/drop。 · regional failover 是否丢 counter。 · client retry 是否重复播放同一 decision。 · fallback slate/pod 是否绕过 cap。

5. Pacing service 如何设计？#

Pacing 是控制系统，不应在 request path 做重计算。

text
Impression / Spend Events
  -> Kafka
  -> Flink aggregates
  -> Budget State Store

Forecast Service
  -> expected future supply

Pacing Optimizer
  -> throttle_rate / bid_multiplier / priority_score
  -> Redis / DynamoDB serving cache

Ad Server
  -> reads pacing knobs

State：

text
LineItemPacingState:
  line_item_id
  goal
  budget
  flight
  delivered
  expected_delivery_now
  remaining_supply
  throttle_rate
  bid_multiplier
  optimizer_version

Senior points：

加 smoothing，防止 optimizer oscillation。 · event lag 会造成 overreaction，要有 freshness guard。 · live event 用 event-specific pacing curve。 · underdelivery explanation 要能落到 targeting、forecast、creative、bid、cap、pacing。

6. Auction mechanics 怎么讲？#

Auction 不是简单最高价。需要同时处理：

direct-sold guaranteed delivery。 · private marketplace / deal ID。 · open auction / DSP bids。 · reserve price / floor。 · pacing / budget。 · policy / brand safety。 · member experience / ad quality。

text
Eligible Direct Campaigns
  -> priority + pacing score

Eligible Programmatic Bids
  -> bid price + deal priority + quality score

Unified Auction / Yield Optimizer
  -> choose winner or pod
  -> log price, reason, losing reasons

Senior answer：

I would separate hard eligibility from economic ranking. Hard constraints such as privacy, policy, creative readiness and frequency cap must be enforced before auction. Then the auction/yield layer can compare direct guaranteed obligations and programmatic yield using priority, pacing, floor price and quality score.

7. Ad pod 生成如何设计？#

Ad podding 是 constrained optimization：

Inputs：

ad break duration。 · candidate ads with duration / campaign / advertiser / category。 · competitive separation rules。 · frequency cap。 · pacing priority。 · creative readiness。 · revenue/score。

text
Candidates
  -> hard filters
  -> duration bucket
  -> category/advertiser separation
  -> score sort
  -> greedy / beam search pod builder
  -> duration alignment check
  -> final pod

Trade-off：

exact optimization 太慢，request path 通常用 greedy/beam/heuristic。 · VOD 可以稍微多算，Live 要更保守。 · pod 里不能只追 revenue，要避免同类/同品牌重复。 · pod duration mismatch 会导致 playback/manifest 问题。

8. Policy enforcement 放在哪里？#

Policy 不是最后一个 if statement。它跨越 serving 和 offline workflow：

campaign creation validation。 · creative approval。 · supply eligibility。 · candidate retrieval。 · targeting/privacy。 · programmatic partner integration。 · reporting/clean room。

Components：

Policy Service / OPA-like engine。 · Policy version store。 · Audit log。 · Policy test suite。 · Decision reason logger。

Senior answer：

I would enforce hard policy before auction and ranking, and log policy version plus rejection reason. Offline jobs should also validate that serving indexes do not contain invalid campaigns or creatives. Policy must be versioned and testable because a bad policy deploy can cause fill-rate drops or compliance issues.

Live Ads / DAI / Streaming Integration#

1. Live ads 为什么难？#

Live ads 是 ads + streaming media + distributed systems 的交汇点。难点：

流量同步尖峰，break 到来时大量用户同时请求。 · ad break 时间动态，不能像 VOD 一样慢慢 retry。 · HLS/DASH manifest、segment boundary、duration alignment 必须正确。 · creative codec/CDN/device compatibility 会影响播放。 · measurement 需要区分 selected、inserted、started、completed。 · 失败时保护播放体验优先于广告收益。

一句话：

In live streaming, playback continuity beats ad optimality.

2. 设计 live DAI 系统#

2.1 Architecture#

text
Live Event Control Plane
  -> event schedule / regions / inventory split
  -> capacity reservation
  -> creative readiness matrix
  -> fallback slate plan

Break Signal Ingestion
  -> SCTE / internal marker / manual trigger
  -> Break Orchestrator
  -> Ad Decisioning / Pod Builder
  -> Manifest Generator
       -> HLS / DASH manifest updates
       -> segment duration alignment
  -> Origin / CDN
  -> Client Playback
  -> Beacons / Kafka / Realtime Dashboard

2.2 Concrete components#

Break Signal Service：接收 SCTE-like marker 或 internal schedule。 · Break Orchestrator：控制每个 live break 的 state machine。 · Ad Decisioning Service：可 per-user，也可 cohort-level 降低 spike。 · Manifest Generator：生成 HLS/DASH ad segments。 · Creative Readiness Cache：Redis/DynamoDB 保存 codec/duration/CDN readiness。 · Origin/CDN：承载 ad media and manifests。 · Realtime Dashboard：break-level latency、fallback、playback error。 · Event Pipeline：client/server beacons 写 Kafka。

2.3 Break state machine#

text
scheduled
  -> prewarm
  -> signal_received
  -> pod_selected
  -> manifest_published
  -> playback_started
  -> beacons_received
  -> finalized

2.4 Senior trade-offs#

per-user ranking vs cohort decisioning：spike 下 cohort 可以保护 latency。 · perfect fill vs playback continuity：宁可 slate，也不能黑屏。 · dynamic decision vs precomputed pod：event 前预计算可降低 break spike。 · measurement freshness vs correctness：live dashboard preliminary，billing finalized。

3. Automated manifest generation 怎么做？#

text
Break Signal
  -> validate event/break id
  -> fetch selected pod
  -> verify creative durations/codecs
  -> build HLS/DASH manifest entries
  -> publish manifest to origin/CDN
  -> emit manifest_published event

关键细节：

segment boundary 必须和播放器 buffer 行为兼容。 · ad creative encoding ladder 要匹配主内容。 · duration mismatch 要有 tolerance 和 fallback。 · manifest publish latency 要分 region/device 监控。 · manifest version 要能追踪到 pod decision/version。

Metrics：

break signal to manifest publish latency。 · manifest generation error rate。 · duration mismatch rate。 · CDN manifest 404/5xx。 · player stall/black screen rate。

4. Live ads 的 fallback 策略是什么？#

Fallback 需要层级化：

text
best personalized pod
  -> precomputed cohort pod
  -> direct-sold guaranteed backup
  -> house ad / promo
  -> slate
  -> continue content

每一层都要提前验证：

creative 是否 approved。 · codec/device 是否兼容。 · CDN 是否 warmed。 · duration 是否匹配。 · measurement 是否能标记 fallback reason。

面试回答：

I would make fallback a first-class product path, not an afterthought. Every live event should have tested fallback slates and dashboards showing fallback rate by region, device and break.

5. Live traffic spike 怎么扩展？#

Live spike 的重点是同步冲击，不是平均 QPS。

准备：

prewarm K8s replicas / autoscaling floor。 · warm Redis caches and campaign indexes。 · pre-position creatives on CDN。 · precompute pods for common cohorts。 · reserve capacity for manifest generator。 · setup war room dashboards。

运行时：

regional partitioning。 · priority queues for live traffic。 · circuit breaker for slow DSP/model dependencies。 · load shedding non-critical personalization。 · cohort-level fallback。

压测：

simulate synchronized break。 · simulate DSP timeout。 · simulate CDN creative miss。 · simulate event pipeline lag。

6. Live measurement 有什么难点？#

Measurement 要区分：

ad selected：server decision。 · ad inserted：manifest included。 · ad started：client playback started。 · quartile/completion：client beacons。 · billable impression：validated event definition。

Pipeline：

text
Client beacons + server insertion events
  -> Kafka
  -> Flink dedup/sessionization
  -> late event handling
  -> realtime live dashboard
  -> finalized reporting/billing

难点：

client 网络差导致 beacon missing。 · server inserted 不等于真实播放。 · device/app version 行为差异。 · high concurrency 放大 duplicate/late event。 · billing 需要 finalized window。

Programmatic / Demand Integration#

1. Programmatic integration 的核心流程是什么？#

1.1 Architecture#

text
Ad Opportunity
  -> Programmatic Gateway
       -> privacy filter / data minimization
       -> bid request builder
       -> partner routing
       -> timeout / circuit breaker
  -> DSP / SSP Partners
  -> Bid Response Validator
       -> price / deal id / creative id
       -> creative approval cache
       -> policy / brand safety
  -> Unified Auction
       -> direct-sold + programmatic
  -> Creative Resolver
  -> Decision Response
  -> Partner / auction logs to Kafka

Core challenge：

external dependency latency。 · data egress/privacy。 · creative quality。 · partner reliability。 · revenue/yield vs playback SLA。

Senior answer：

Programmatic demand is valuable but opportunistic. It should never make the playback path hostage to a slow partner. I would isolate it behind a partner gateway with timeouts, circuit breakers, partner scorecards and pre-approved creative caches.

2. Bid request 需要包含什么？#

Bid request 字段：

text
BidRequest:
  request_id
  inventory_source
  content_context:
    content_category, rating, live/vod, brand_safety_labels
  device_context:
    device_type, app/platform, screen, codec support
  geo_context:
    country, region, timezone
  ad_slot:
    format, pod_duration, position, allowed_durations
  deal_info:
    deal_id, floor_price, priority
  privacy:
    consent, data_sharing_flags, limited_ad_tracking
  identity/signals:
    only privacy-approved coarse signals

不要把 member-level sensitive data 直接出站。要有：

partner allowlist。 · field-level policy。 · data minimization。 · audit log。 · per-partner contract/purpose limits。

3. DSP timeout 怎么处理？#

DSP timeout 是 programmatic 系统的核心面试点。

text
Partner Gateway
  -> per-partner timeout budget
  -> async fanout
  -> collect bids until deadline
  -> drop late bids
  -> circuit breaker if timeout/error high
  -> partner scorecard

Policy：

hard deadline，late bid 不参与 auction。 · partner timeout/error rate 高时降权或熔断。 · fallback 到 direct-sold/house demand。 · 记录 no-bid、timeout、invalid bid、creative reject。

Metrics：

partner p95/p99 latency。 · bid rate。 · timeout rate。 · win rate。 · revenue by partner。 · invalid creative rate。 · auction participation loss。

模板：

External demand is opportunistic; playback SLO is mandatory. I would rather lose a late bid than delay the ad decision path.

4. Creative approval 在 programmatic 中怎么做？#

Programmatic creative 不能在实时 path 临时下载、转码、审核。

text
Partner Creative Submission
  -> creative registry
  -> malware / policy / brand safety scan
  -> transcode / codec validation
  -> device compatibility test
  -> reviewer / automated approval
  -> creative approval cache
  -> serving readiness index

实时 bid response 只允许：

creative_id 已知。 · approval_status = approved。 · cdn_status = ready。 · duration/format/device compatible。 · policy version valid。

未知 creative：

reject。 · limited sandbox path。 · async review，不参与当前 auction。

5. Direct-sold 和 programmatic 如何共同竞争？#

这是 marketplace design，不是简单排序。

text
Direct Guaranteed Demand
  -> delivery obligation
  -> pacing pressure
  -> priority tier

Programmatic Demand
  -> bid price
  -> deal priority
  -> partner quality

Yield / Allocation Engine
  -> protect guaranteed delivery
  -> respect floor/reserve
  -> maximize yield when safe
  -> enforce member experience

Senior trade-offs：

guaranteed delivery 影响长期 trust，不能总被 open auction 抢走。 · programmatic 提高 yield，但质量、latency、creative risk 更高。 · pacing pressure 高的 direct campaign 可以提升 priority。 · member experience constraints 是硬约束，不参与价格竞价。

回答模板：

I would not let the highest bid always win. Direct-sold guaranteed campaigns have delivery obligations, while programmatic demand gives yield upside. The decision should combine eligibility, pacing pressure, reserve price, bid value, partner reliability and member-experience constraints.

Eventing / Data Quality / Reporting#

1. Ads Eventing 的核心设计是什么？#

事件来源包括 client beacons、server-side ad insertion、ad decision logs、programmatic partner callbacks、billing events。Pipeline 要做 validation、dedup、enrichment、privacy filtering、schema evolution、routing 到 realtime/warehouse/billing/measurement。每个事件要有 event_id、request_id、session_id、ad_id、campaign_id、timestamp、source、schema_version。核心是 data fidelity。

2. 为什么 ads events 很难？#

因为事件多源、异步、可能丢失/重复/乱序，且 downstream 影响重大。一个 impression event 错，可能影响 frequency cap、reporting、measurement、billing、pacing。Client 设备网络不稳定，server events 和 client events 不完全等价。需要 reconciliation 和 quality checks，而不是简单 Kafka pipe。

3. 如何做 dedup？#

每个 event 生成稳定 id，例如 request_id + ad_id + event_type + timestamp bucket。Pipeline 使用 state store 或 warehouse merge 去重。要处理 retry、client resend、server duplicate、partner duplicate。Dedup 规则必须按 event type 设计：impression、quartile、click、conversion、billing event 语义不同。

4. Late events 怎么处理？#

定义 watermark 和 allowed lateness。Realtime decisioning 可能用 near-real-time counts，billing/reporting 可以等待更长窗口。Late events 进入 correction pipeline，更新 reporting aggregates 和 reconciliation。要向用户/advertiser 说明 reporting latency 和 finalization window。广告报告常有 preliminary vs finalized 数据。

5. Reporting platform 如何设计？#

Validated events 进入 warehouse/lakehouse，aggregate service 生成 campaign metrics，API/UI 支持 slice by campaign、line item、creative、geo、device、time。需要 access control、data freshness、metric definitions、export、rate limits。报告必须 accredited、timely、easy to consume。核心不是画图，而是 metric correctness 和 advertiser trust。

6. Metric definitions 为什么重要？#

Impression、viewable impression、completion、reach、frequency、spend、conversion 都必须有统一定义。不同团队如果各算各的，会造成 advertiser report、billing、measurement 不一致。需要 metrics layer 或 semantic layer，版本化定义和 change management。面试中可以强调：ads business runs on trusted metrics。

7. Billing data pipeline 有什么特殊要求？#

Billing 要准确、合规、可审计。它需要合同/pricing、delivery events、makegoods、credits、adjustments、currency/tax、invoice state。不能像普通 analytics 那样随便重算不留痕。需要 immutable ledger-like records、approval workflow、reconciliation reports。Finance 系统关注 correctness over freshness。

Identity / Privacy / Clean Room#

1. Ads Identity Graph 如何理解？#

Identity Graph 把 account、profile、household、device、partner IDs、advertiser IDs 等信号在隐私约束下关联起来。它支持 frequency cap、reach measurement、audience matching、conversion attribution。CTV 场景中 household identity 很重要，但也更敏感。Identity 设计要兼顾 coverage、accuracy、privacy、consent。

2. Deterministic 和 probabilistic matching 区别？#

Deterministic matching 基于明确标识，如 login、hashed email、device/account link，准确但覆盖有限。Probabilistic matching 用行为、设备、网络、统计模型推断，覆盖高但误差和隐私风险更大。neo 这种会员平台有强 first-party identity，但和 advertiser/partner matching 仍需要隐私保护和 policy。面试里要说：identity confidence should be explicit。

3. Custom Audience ingestion 如何设计？#

Advertiser 上传 hashed identifiers 或 audience files，系统做 schema validation、encryption、privacy checks、matching、audience segment creation。要有 upload API、status、match rate reporting、retention、delete support、access control。不能把 raw advertiser data 暴露给 ad serving；serving 只使用 privacy-safe segment membership 或 eligibility signals。

4. Conversion data ingestion 如何设计？#

Advertiser/partner 发送 conversion events，包含 conversion_id、timestamp、value、hashed identifiers、campaign metadata。Pipeline 做 validation、dedup、identity match、attribution windows、privacy aggregation。Conversion data 用于 measurement、optimization、retargeting。要防 replay、fraud、PII leakage、schema drift。

5. Data Clean Room 怎么设计？#

Clean room 允许双方在受控环境中 join/aggregate 数据，但不导出 user-level rows。需要 query templates、aggregation thresholds、privacy budget、access control、audit logs、partner isolation、result review。支持 use cases：audience overlap、incrementality、conversion measurement、reach/frequency。面试里强调 clean room is a governed computation environment, not a shared database。

6. 隐私和 targeting 如何平衡？#

Targeting 越细，效果越好但隐私风险越高。系统要做 data minimization、purpose limitation、consent、segment thresholds、sensitive category exclusion、aggregation。Contextual targeting 和 content metadata 可以减少对个人身份的依赖。neo 的 deep content metadata 是差异化优势：可以做 brand-safe contextual ads，同时保护用户隐私。

Signals / Targeting / Audience#

1. Targeting 系统输入有哪些？#

输入包括 member/account signals、device、geo、content metadata、time、language、plan、ad exposure history、custom audiences、contextual categories、forecasted inventory、privacy flags。每个 signal 都要有 freshness、source、allowed use、confidence。Serving path 不能临时做重计算，需要预计算或低延迟 lookup。

2. Contextual targeting 为什么重要？#

Contextual targeting 基于内容上下文而非个人身份。neo 有丰富内容 metadata，可以支持 genre、mood、rating、theme、language、live event context。它对 brand safety 和 privacy 都友好。比如某 advertiser 想投 sports live event 或 family-friendly content，可以通过 contextual rules 实现。

3. Probabilistic audience modeling 怎么讲？#

当确定性 audience 覆盖不足时，可以用 ML 根据 signals 预测用户属于某 audience 或可能 conversion 的概率。Serving 时将概率作为 targeting/ranking feature，而不是硬标签。要评估 calibration、bias、privacy、segment size、outcome lift。面试中强调 probabilistic signals need confidence and guardrails。

4. Retargeting audience 如何构建？#

基于 engagement/conversion signals 创建 audience，例如看过某广告、访问过 advertiser site、完成转化、未完成购买。Pipeline 要 ingest events、dedup、identity match、apply lookback window、生成 segment membership。Retargeting 很强但隐私敏感，需要 consent、frequency cap、exclusion logic。CTV 中还要避免体验过度追踪感。

5. Targeting eligibility 如何在实时服务中使用？#

不要实时扫描复杂 audience。应预计算 audience membership 或 compact segment index，在 ad request 时低延迟 lookup。Eligibility filter 返回 candidate line items。更新需要 near-real-time，但可以接受分钟级延迟，取决于 use case。关键是 serving index 和 audience pipeline 的一致性。

Forecasting / Simulation / Planning#

1. Ads forecasting 预测什么？#

预测未来 inventory、available impressions、reach/frequency、campaign delivery、ad break supply、audience availability、pricing/yield。它支持 media planning、sales commitments、pacing、pricing、inventory split。neo 还要考虑 content schedule、live events、regional viewing patterns、device mix。Forecasting 是 ads business 的承诺引擎。

2. Near-realtime forecasting service 怎么设计？#

输入最新 viewing/inventory signals、campaign bookings、supply constraints、seasonality、live schedules。服务输出可查询的 availability 和 delivery forecast。架构上有 batch baseline + streaming corrections；API 支持 planners/DSP/ad ops 查询。要记录 forecast version、input snapshot、model version。因为业务会基于 forecast 做承诺，reproducibility 很重要。

3. Offline simulation 系统怎么设计？#

Simulation 用历史/合成流量回放 ad decisioning，评估新策略、pricing、pacing、allocation。需要 traffic logs、campaign configs、decisioning code/model version、simulator engine、metrics output。难点是 counterfactual：历史没有看到未投广告的结果。Simulation 适合 scenario planning，但上线前仍需 controlled experiments。

4. Yield optimization 是什么？#

Yield optimization 让有限 inventory 产生更高长期价值。它考虑 price、demand、guaranteed contracts、programmatic bids、member experience、future opportunity cost。不是每次卖最高价，而是全局优化。Forecasting、pricing、pacing、allocation 共同决定 yield。

5. Media planning 系统如何设计？#

Advertiser 输入目标：budget、audience、reach/frequency、flight、geo、content/context、KPI。系统调用 forecasting 和 pricing，生成可执行 media plan：inventory package、line items、expected delivery、cost、constraints。需要 what-if scenario、availability check、compliance、approval、handoff to OMS。这里很适合讲 product + optimization + data systems。

6. Pricing engine 如何设计？#

Pricing engine 管 rate cards、reserve prices、discounts、packages、seasonality、inventory scarcity、deal terms。实时场景下返回 floor/reserve/bid guidance，规划场景下返回 quote。需要 versioning、approval、audit、effective dates。Pricing 错误直接影响收入，所以要 strong governance 和 testing。

Creatives / GenAI / Modular Formats#

1. Ad Creatives Infrastructure 核心是什么？#

它支撑 advertiser creative asset 从上传、处理、审核、预览、批准、分发到 serving。需要处理多格式、多设备、多 ad units、brand safety、policy、transcoding、metadata、creative readiness。GenAI 会增加 creative cardinality 和 modularity，infra 必须支持大量变体和快速审核。

2. Creative lifecycle 怎么设计？#

状态包括 draft、uploaded、processing、validated、reviewing、approved、active、rejected、archived。每个 creative 有 advertiser、campaign、format、duration、codec、language、policy status、asset URI、thumbnail、preview URL、approval history。Serving 只能使用 approved + compatible creatives。Creative lifecycle 是 ads serving correctness 的前置条件。

3. Modular ad formats 是什么？#

Modular ad formats 把广告拆成组件：video、image、copy、CTA、logo、interactive element、localization variant。系统可以按 device/context/member 组合不同模块。好处是 personalization 和 creative scale；挑战是组合爆炸、approval、brand consistency、measurement attribution。GenAI 可以生成模块，但 policy 和 QA 必须跟上。

4. GenAI creative workflow 如何设计？#

Advertiser 输入 brief、brand kit、assets、target audience、format。系统生成 creative variants，做 brand/policy/safety checks，提供 preview，进入 approval，再分发到 ad decisioning。每个 variant 记录 prompt、model version、source assets、rights、approval。成功指标是 creative quality、approval rate、time-to-launch、performance lift、cost per approved creative。

5. Creative preview 为什么重要？#

广告在 TV、mobile、web 上呈现不同，preview 要模拟 device、aspect ratio、safe area、ad break context、interactive behavior。Preview 能减少上线后 creative error。系统要保证 preview 和 production serving 使用同一 creative metadata/renderer，避免 “preview 好，线上坏”。这是 creative tooling 的关键可靠性问题。

6. Creative distribution 到 serving 怎么做？#

Approved creative 需要转码成多 bitrate/format，推送到 CDN/origin，更新 serving index，确认 readiness。Ad decisioning 只选择 ready creative。要有 readiness checks：asset exists、encoding complete、policy approved、metadata valid、CDN warm。否则实时选择到不可播放 creative 会破坏体验。

GenAI / Agentic Workflows for Ads#

1. Ads 中 GenAI 能做什么？#

可以用于 creative generation、copy variants、localization、campaign setup assistance、seller insights、media plan explanation、report summary、anomaly explanation、ad ops automation、audience recommendations、forecast Q&A。关键是把 GenAI 嵌入 workflow，而不是做一个旁边的 chatbot。每个输出要可审核、可追溯、可撤销。

2. Sales agents 怎么设计？#

Sales agent 读取 account data、opportunity、past spend、inventory forecast、campaign performance，给 seller 推荐 next best action、proposal、talking points、package。它要遵守 CRM permissions 和 data boundaries。高风险动作如发送 proposal、改价格、提交 order 需要 human approval。指标是 seller productivity、pipeline conversion、time saved、recommendation adoption。

3. Ad ops agent 怎么设计？#

Ad ops agent 可以帮助检查 campaign setup：line item dates、budget、targeting conflicts、creative readiness、forecast underdelivery、policy issues。它输出 checklist 和 suggested fixes，必要时调用 OMS APIs。默认不自动修改生产 campaign，除非 low-risk 且可回滚。这个 agent 的价值在减少人工配置错误。

4. Live ads agent 怎么设计？#

Live event 中 agent 可以监控 break schedule、inventory, fill, pacing, manifest status, creative readiness，提前提示风险，并在规则允许下调整 traffic shaping 或 fallback。因为 live 风险高，agent 应是 assistive first，human-in-the-loop。所有建议要带 evidence 和 confidence。指标是 incident avoidance、faster response、fill/revenue improvement。

5. GenAI foundations 平台需要什么？#

需要 prompt/model registry、tool registry、RAG over ads knowledge/entity graph、evaluation framework、safety/policy, access control、logging、cost tracking、feedback loop。Ads organization 很多 teams 可以复用这层：sales, reporting, creative, ad ops, planning。平台价值在 consistency 和 governance。

6. Ads Entity Service / knowledge graph 能做什么？#

把 advertiser、agency、account、campaign、order、line item、creative、audience、inventory、content、pricing、invoice 等实体连接起来。它支持 search、agent reasoning、access control、impact analysis、reporting drill-down。面试里可以说：knowledge graph turns scattered ads domain objects into a coherent operating model。

Account / OMS / Sales Workflow#

1. Account Foundations 为什么重要？#

广告业务需要权威定义：advertiser 是谁、agency 关系、brand hierarchy、eligibility、payment/credit、spend attribution。没有 authoritative accounts domain，下游 campaign、billing、reporting、sales incentive 都会不一致。Account Foundations 是 neo Ads Suite 的 business identity layer。

2. Account data model 怎么设计？#

实体包括 advertiser、brand、agency、holding company、account team、billing entity、eligibility status、contracts、regions、permissions。需要 hierarchy、merge/split、lifecycle、audit。外部信号用于 enrichment，但 source of truth 要清楚。Account changes 会影响 campaign booking、reporting、billing、access control。

3. OMS 如何设计？#

OMS 管 order、line item、flight、budget、targeting、creative requirements、approval、status。它与 forecasting 检查 availability，与 pricing 生成 quote，与 campaign delivery 同步投放状态，与 finance 做 billing。OMS 要强 audit、versioning、approval workflow，因为它承载合同和收入。不是普通 CRUD。

4. Campaign hierarchy 变更有什么风险？#

如果 line item targeting、budget、flight、creative 修改没有版本和审计，会影响 delivery、reporting、billing。需要 effective time、change log、approval、validation、forecast recheck。Active campaign 的修改要谨慎，可能需要 re-pacing 和 advertiser notification。面试里强调 change management。

5. Incentive compensation 系统有什么难点？#

Sales incentive 依赖 revenue attribution、account ownership、territory、quota、product type、adjustments。规则复杂且影响薪酬，必须准确、可审计、可解释。AI 可以帮助 seller insights，但 compensation calculation 本身要 deterministic 和 governed。这个点适合展示 finance/business systems 思维。

Supply / Inventory Split / External Publishers#

1. External supply integration 的核心挑战是什么？#

要把 third-party TV publishers/broadcasters 的 content 和 ad inventory 接入 neo，同时满足 inventory split、rights、publisher demand、neo demand、reporting、reconciliation。每个 partner 可能有不同 contract terms 和 technical integration。系统要把复杂商业条件转成 policy/decisioning rules。

2. Inventory split 怎么设计？#

Inventory split 定义某 supply 的 ad opportunities 如何在 neo demand、publisher demand、direct/programmatic 等之间分配。可以按 percentage、priority、time、region、content、deal terms。Decisioning 时先判断 supply owner 和 split rules，再进入 demand selection。需要 audit：为什么这个 slot 给了哪一方。错误会影响 partner trust 和 revenue share。

3. Supply eligibility 是什么？#

不是所有 inventory 都能给所有 ad。Eligibility 受内容 rights、region、member plan、device、publisher rules、brand safety、ad format、technical readiness 影响。Supply eligibility 系统决定哪些 ad opportunities 可 monetized，以及可被哪些 demand 使用。它是 targeting 的 supply-side counterpart。

4. Partner reporting 如何设计？#

External publishers 需要看到自己 supply 上的 delivery、fill、revenue share、ad quality、errors。Reporting 要按 contract terms 聚合，并和 finance reconciliation 一致。要处理 partner-specific metrics 和 data sharing policy。不能把 neo member-level data 直接暴露给 partner。

Measurement / Attribution / Incrementality#

1. Reach 和 frequency 怎么计算？#

Reach 是去重后看到广告的人/household 数，frequency 是平均曝光次数。难点是 identity resolution、cross-device、household/account、late/dedup events。报告中要定义 reach window 和 identity basis。CTV 场景常按 household 或 account 汇总，不一定是个人。

2. Brand lift 怎么理解？#

Brand lift 衡量广告对 awareness/favorability/intent 的影响，通常通过 exposed vs control surveys 或实验方法。上下文里提到 8X higher brand favorability than CTV average，这类指标说明 premium streaming environment 对品牌效果有价值。面试时不要过度解释具体数字来源，重点讲 measurement system 要能支持可信实验和报告。

3. Conversion attribution 怎么设计？#

需要曝光事件、转化事件、identity match、attribution window、dedup、credit assignment。可用 last-touch、multi-touch、incrementality 等方法。Privacy 约束下，结果可能需要聚合和 clean room。Attribution 系统要可解释：为什么某 conversion 被归到某 campaign。

4. Incrementality 为什么重要？#

Attribution 可能把本来就会发生的转化算给广告；incrementality 关心广告带来的因果增量。方法包括 holdout、geo experiments、synthetic control、PSM 等。对高端 advertisers，incrementality 更能证明价值。系统要支持实验分组、exposure control、measurement pipeline。

5. Measurement 和 optimization 如何闭环？#

Measurement 输出 conversion/lift/performance signals，进入 optimization models、pacing、budget allocation、audience modeling。闭环要防 feedback bias：系统只看到自己选择投放的样本。需要 exploration、experiments、clean labels。面试里可说：measurement is not just reporting; it is the learning signal for decisioning。

Finance / Billing / Revenue Recognition#

1. Ads Finance 平台为什么复杂？#

广告收入来自 direct, programmatic, sponsorship, inventory split, revenue share, makegoods, credits。计费依赖 contract、pricing、delivery、adjustments、currency/tax。Revenue recognition 还要符合会计规则。系统需要准确、自动化、可审计，不能用临时 SQL 拼。Finance correctness 是 business trust。

2. Billing pipeline 怎么设计？#

Inputs：validated delivery events、pricing terms、campaign/order metadata、partner contracts、adjustments。Pipeline 计算 billable units、amount、tax/currency、invoice line items，进入 approval/invoicing/journal。每一步保存 immutable records 和 audit trail。Reporting 与 invoice 不一致时要能 reconciliation。

3. Makegood / credit 怎么处理？#

如果 campaign underdelivers 或出现服务问题，可能给 advertiser makegood 或 credit。系统要记录 reason、approval、amount、关联 campaign/invoice。它影响 revenue recognition 和 sales relationship。不能让 makegood 变成手工 Excel；需要 workflow 和 audit。

4. Financial reconciliation 怎么做？#

比对 ad server delivery、reporting aggregates、programmatic partner reports、invoice records、payments。差异要分类：late events、dedup change、partner discrepancy、pricing config、currency。Finance 平台需要 reconciliation dashboard 和 exception workflow。面试里强调：billing pipeline needs deterministic, explainable computation。

Ads System Design 题库#

这一节要按 senior SWE / MLE system design 来准备。不要只列组件。面试官真正想看的是：你能不能在 latency、revenue、member experience、advertiser outcome、data correctness、privacy、ML optimization 之间做清楚 trade-off。

0. Senior 答题总框架#

每一道 ads system design 都用这个顺序：

  1. clarify requirements：online/offline、latency、scale、business goal、user experience、privacy。
  2. define entities：campaign、line item、creative、ad break、member/profile、event、budget、forecast。
  3. draw serving path：request -> eligibility -> ranking/auction -> decision -> delivery -> event logging。
  4. draw data/control path：campaign config、index build、budget updates、model training、reporting、billing。
  5. state + consistency：哪些必须强一致，哪些可以 eventual consistency。
  6. ML/MLE layer：candidate generation、ranking、calibration、feedback loop、offline/online eval。
  7. failure mode：timeout、stale index、partner outage、event loss、overdelivery、bad creative。
  8. metrics：p95/p99 latency、fill rate、delivery ratio、revenue/yield、QoE、event lag、billing discrepancy。
  9. debuggability：decision reason、trace id、rejected reason、index version、model version、config version。

一句高级开场：

I would design ads systems as two planes: a low-latency online decisioning plane and a correctness-oriented data/control plane. The online path must be fast, resilient and explainable; the data plane must produce reliable budgets, forecasts, reports, measurement and billing. The hardest part is not one service, but keeping decisioning, events, optimization and finance consistent enough under real-time constraints.

0A. Normal System Design Components：面试必须说出的具体组件#

面试里不要只说 “cache / queue / database”。Senior 答案要能把抽象组件落到具体 infra：Kafka、Redis、DynamoDB/Cassandra、S3、Iceberg/Delta、Flink/Spark、ClickHouse/Druid/Pinot、Elasticsearch/OpenSearch、Kubernetes、Triton/Ray Serve、CDN、OpenTelemetry。

0A.1 Global ad serving：在线请求路径#

text
CTV / Mobile / Web Client
  -> CDN / Edge / WAF
  -> API Gateway / Envoy / ALB
  -> Ad Request Gateway
       -> Request Context Builder
            -> Identity Service
            -> Targeting / Privacy Service
            -> Content Metadata Service
       -> Candidate Retrieval
            -> Campaign Serving Index
               (Elasticsearch / OpenSearch / custom in-memory index)
       -> Eligibility / Policy Engine
            -> Frequency Cap Store
               (Redis Cluster / DynamoDB / Cassandra)
            -> Pacing State Cache
               (Redis / DynamoDB / Cassandra)
            -> Creative Readiness Index
               (Redis / DynamoDB + S3 metadata)
       -> ML Scoring / Ranking
            -> Feature Store Online
               (Redis / Cassandra / DynamoDB)
            -> Model Serving
               (Triton / Ray Serve / KServe)
       -> Auction / Podding
       -> Creative Resolver
            -> S3 / Object Store
            -> CDN creative URLs
       -> Decision Response
       -> Decision Log
            -> Kafka / Kinesis / Pulsar

这张图回答 global ad server、ad decisioning service、frequency cap、pacing、ML serving 都能用。关键点：

  • Redis：低延迟 counters、pacing state、feature cache、short TTL decision state。
  • DynamoDB/Cassandra：高 QPS key-value state，适合 cap/budget/serving metadata。
  • Elasticsearch/OpenSearch：campaign/candidate retrieval，适合 filter-heavy query。
  • Triton/Ray Serve/KServe：ranking model / calibration model / creative quality model serving。
  • S3 + CDN：creative assets、transcoded media、preview artifacts、offline snapshots。
  • Kafka/Kinesis/Pulsar：decision log、impression events、click/conversion events、budget updates。

0A.2 Data / event / reporting path#

text
Client Beacons / Server Events / Partner Events
  -> Event Gateway
       -> schema validation
       -> auth / consent / privacy filtering
  -> Kafka / Kinesis / Pulsar topics
       -> ads.requests
       -> ads.decisions
       -> ads.impressions
       -> ads.completions
       -> ads.clicks
       -> ads.conversions
  -> Stream Processing
       -> Flink / Spark Structured Streaming
       -> dedup
       -> enrichment
       -> late event handling
       -> quality checks
  -> Online feedback sinks
       -> Redis / DynamoDB counters for caps/pacing
       -> Feature Store online
  -> Analytics sinks
       -> ClickHouse / Pinot / Druid for dashboard OLAP
       -> S3 Data Lake + Iceberg / Delta / Hudi
       -> Snowflake / BigQuery / Redshift warehouse
  -> Batch / ML
       -> Spark / dbt / Airflow / Argo
       -> Feature Store offline
       -> Training datasets
       -> Model registry

这张图回答 ads eventing pipeline、reporting API、billing reconciliation、ML optimization 很有用。高级点：

online path 追求 low latency，data path 追求 correctness + replayability。 · Kafka topic 要按 event_type、region、tenant/partner、campaign shard 分区。 · S3/lakehouse 保存原始、validated、enriched、finalized 多层数据。 · OLAP store 服务 dashboard，不要让 API 每次扫 raw events。 · billing/reporting 要有 preliminary 和 finalized window。

0A.3 Component selection cheat sheet#

NeedCommon componentsWhy / trade-off
edge trafficCDN, WAF, ALB/NLB, Envoy, API Gatewayabsorb traffic, route by region, protect origin
service runtimeKubernetes, ECS, service mesh, gRPC/RESTautoscaling, rollout, mTLS, observability
low-latency cacheRedis Cluster, Memcachedfrequency cap, pacing state, feature cache

0A.4 每道 Ads system design 的组件栈#
