0A.5 面试时怎么自然说组件#

不要这样说：

I will use Kafka, Redis and S3.

要这样说：

I would use Redis only for low-latency serving state such as frequency counters, pacing knobs and feature cache; Kafka for durable event streams such as decisions, impressions and conversions; and S3/Iceberg for replayable historical truth. The ad decision path should not scan S3 or OLAP tables. It reads precomputed indexes and caches, then writes decision logs asynchronously to Kafka.

1. 设计一个 global ad server#

1.1 Clarify requirements#

先问清楚：

  • traffic type：VOD、Live、CTV、mobile/web，是否支持 external publishers。
  • latency budget：ad decision 通常要在几十毫秒到一两百毫秒内完成，live/manifest path 更紧。
  • global scale：多 region active-active，local serving，central control plane。
  • demand type：direct-sold、programmatic、house ads、sponsorship、guaranteed delivery。
  • formats：pre-roll、mid-roll、ad pod、pause ad、interactive/ad variants。
  • constraints：frequency cap、pacing、targeting、brand safety、creative readiness、privacy。
  • success metrics：fill rate、revenue/yield、delivery SLA、p95/p99 latency、member QoE、ad error rate。

1.2 High-level architecture#

text
Client / Playback / Manifest Service
  -> Ad Request Gateway
  -> Request Context Builder
       -> member/profile/privacy flags
       -> content/ad-break context
       -> device/geo/time signals
  -> Candidate Retrieval
       -> serving index
       -> deal/priority filters
       -> creative readiness filter
  -> Policy / Eligibility Engine
       -> targeting
       -> brand safety
       -> frequency cap
       -> pacing/budget eligibility
  -> ML Scoring / Auction / Podding
  -> Creative Resolver
  -> Decision Response
  -> Event Logger / Decision Trace

Control/data plane：

text
Campaign Management / OMS
  -> validation
  -> campaign metadata store
  -> index builder
  -> regional serving index

Event Pipeline
  -> impression/click/completion events
  -> budget/pacing aggregates
  -> reporting/billing/measurement
  -> ML training datasets

1.3 Core API#

text
POST /adDecision
Request:
  request_id
  member_context: profile_id, household_id, privacy_flags, consent
  content_context: title_id, genre, rating, live_event_id
  playback_context: device_type, app_version, ad_break_id, pod_duration
  location_context: country, region, timezone
  supply_context: inventory_source, publisher_id, format

Response:
  decision_id
  pod:
    - ad_id
      creative_id
      duration_ms
      media_url
      tracking_urls
      click_through / interactive metadata
  fallback_reason
  decision_reasons
  model_version
  index_version

1.4 Data model#

text
Campaign
  campaign_id
  advertiser_id
  objective
  budget
  flight_start / flight_end
  targeting_spec
  brand_safety_spec
  pacing_policy

LineItem
  line_item_id
  campaign_id
  priority
  deal_id
  goal_type: impression | reach | completion | conversion
  bid / price
  delivery_goal

Creative
  creative_id
  advertiser_id
  duration
  codec
  approval_status
  device_compatibility
  cdn_status

DecisionLog
  request_id
  decision_id
  candidates_considered
  rejected_reasons
  selected_ads
  model_version
  latency_breakdown
  index_version

1.5 Senior trade-offs#

ProblemSenior answer
low latency vs correctnessServing path uses precomputed indexes and caches; correctness corrections happen through event/reconciliation pipelines.
global availability vs consistent caps/budgetRegional serving with local state and periodic/global reconciliation; hard caps may use stronger coordination for high-value campaigns.
member experience vs revenueUse QoE constraints: max ads/hour, category separation, creative quality, fallback to slate/content instead of risky ad.
direct-sold vs programmaticUnified candidate interface but different latency/reliability policies; programmatic can timeout and lose auction.
ML ranking vs explainabilityModel can score candidates, but final decision must log constraints, filters and business reason codes.

1.6 MLE layer#

Ad server 不只是 rules engine：

  • candidate retrieval：根据 targeting/coarse partition 取候选，避免全量 campaign scan。
  • ranking model：预测 completion、conversion、negative feedback、member fatigue、bid-adjusted value。
  • calibration：不同 region/device/content 的 score 要校准，否则 auction/ranking 偏。
  • exploration：新 campaign/creative 冷启动要有 controlled exploration。
  • feedback loop：impression、completion、skip、conversion、complaint、creative error 回流训练。
  • online features：frequency、recent exposure、content context、device、time-of-day、campaign pacing state。

1.7 Failure modes / runbook#

  • campaign index stale：新 campaign 不 serve 或旧 campaign 继续 serve。看 index version、build lag、regional publish status。
  • frequency cap store slow：fallback 到 conservative cap 或 reduce personalized demand。
  • pacing service outage：使用 last known throttle，保护 budget 不 overdeliver。
  • ML scoring timeout：fallback to rule-based score / cached score。
  • creative CDN issue：exclude creative and select backup/slate。
  • event logger lag：serving 可以继续，但 budget/reporting freshness degraded，要标记 risk。

1.8 Metrics#

  • Serving：QPS、p50/p95/p99 latency、timeout rate、error rate。
  • Marketplace：fill rate、eligible candidates/request、win rate、yield、revenue/minute。
  • Experience：ad start failure、black screen、repeated ad rate、ad load delay、completion rate。
  • Correctness：over-cap rate、overdelivery/underdelivery、stale index rate、decision trace coverage。
  • ML：AUC/calibration、online lift、model timeout rate、feature freshness。

1.9 你可以怎么结合经历讲#

我会把它类比成 real-time bidding / ML serving 系统：在线路径要非常短，所有重计算都要前置到 serving index、feature cache 和 model cache。过去做 100B+ opportunities/day 级别系统时，我学到最重要的是：低延迟服务必须有 reason code、budget/cap state、event feedback 和 rollback path，否则出了 fill rate 或 revenue 问题很难定位。

2. 设计 frequency capping system#

2.1 问题本质#

Frequency capping 是 ads 里最容易暴露系统成熟度的问题。它保护 member experience，也保护 advertiser 的有效触达。难点不是简单计数，而是：

identity 不稳定：profile、household、device、logged-out、partner ID。 · event 可能丢失或 late arrival。 · 多 region serving 并发更新。 · cap hierarchy 复杂：creative、campaign、advertiser、category、industry。 · 在线路径 latency 很紧，不能每次做强一致分布式事务。

2.2 Requirements#

支持 N impressions per X hours/days。 · 支持多层 cap：creative/campaign/advertiser/category。 · 支持 VOD/live/CTV/device cross-device。 · read/write path 低延迟。 · 支持 privacy/consent，不可非法 join identity。 · 有 over-cap monitoring 和 correction。

2.3 Architecture#

text
Ad Decision Service
  -> Identity Resolver
  -> Frequency Cap Service
       -> hot counter cache
       -> regional counter store
       -> cap policy store
       -> event correction consumer
  -> Decision

Impression Events
  -> Event Pipeline
  -> Dedup / Validation
  -> Cap Correction Stream
  -> Counter Store Reconciliation

2.4 Data model#

text
CapPolicy:
  scope: creative | campaign | advertiser | category
  entity_id
  identity_scope: profile | household | device
  max_count
  window_type: rolling | calendar
  window_size

CapCounter:
  cap_key = identity_key + scope + entity_id + window_bucket
  count
  last_updated_at
  confidence
  source: server_decision | client_impression | validated_event
  ttl

2.5 Serving path#

  1. request 进来，identity resolver 返回 identity keys 和 confidence。
  2. 对候选 campaign/creative 批量查询 cap counters。
  3. 超 cap 的 candidate 被过滤，reason code 写 frequency_cap_exceeded。
  4. 选中广告后写 provisional exposure。
  5. validated impression event 到达后做 correction。

2.6 Consistency trade-off#

ChoiceProsCons
strong global counterover-cap 最少latency 高，跨 region 风险大
regional local counter快，可用性高cross-region duplicate exposure
provisional + correctionpractical需要 reconciliation 和 over-cap dashboard

Senior 答法：

I would not put a strongly consistent global transaction in the critical path for every ad request. I would use regional low-latency counters with TTL, provisional writes, validated-event correction, and monitoring for over-cap. For premium campaigns or strict legal caps, I can selectively use stronger coordination.

2.7 MLE / product nuance#

频控不只是硬规则，也影响 ranking。接近 cap 的用户可以降低 score。 · ad fatigue model 可以预测重复广告带来的 negative engagement。 · 对新 campaign 需要 exploration，但不能牺牲 cap。 · identity confidence 低时要更保守，避免 repeated ad。

2.8 Debug / incident#

用户看到重复广告：

查 request_id/session_id/ad_id 是否 server 重复选择。 · 查 client retry 是否重复播放同一个 decision。 · 查 identity key 是否 profile/device 切换。 · 查 event lag 是否导致 cap counter 没更新。 · 查 regional failover 是否丢失 local counter。 · 查 fallback pod 是否绕过 cap。

2.9 Metrics#

over-cap rate。 · cap lookup p95/p99 latency。 · counter write failure rate。 · validated impression lag。 · identity resolution coverage。 · repeated ad complaint rate。 · cap correction delta。

3. 设计 pacing and budget optimizer#

3.1 问题本质#

Pacing 决定 campaign 在 flight 中如何花预算/交付 impressions。它是 ads marketplace 的控制系统。太快会 overdeliver / early spendout，太慢会 underdeliver / lose revenue。

3.2 Requirements#

支持 guaranteed delivery、budget-based delivery、performance goal。 · 平滑 delivery，不要第一天花完预算。 · 根据 forecast supply、win rate、performance 动态调整。 · 支持 live events 这种 supply spike。 · 输出可被 ad server 低延迟读取。 · 可解释：为什么 underdelivery/overdelivery。

3.3 Architecture#

text
Events Stream
  -> Streaming Aggregator
       -> delivered impressions/spend/completions
       -> per line item counters
  -> Budget State Store

Forecast Service
  -> future supply estimate
  -> target availability

Pacing Optimizer
  -> compute delivery gap
  -> compute throttle / priority / bid multiplier
  -> write Serving Pacing Cache

Ad Server
  -> reads pacing state
  -> filters/throttles/ranks candidates

3.4 Data model#

text
LineItemBudgetState:
  line_item_id
  total_budget
  flight_start
  flight_end
  delivered_impressions
  delivered_spend
  target_curve
  expected_delivery_now
  delivery_gap
  throttle_rate
  bid_multiplier
  priority_score
  last_optimizer_version

3.5 Algorithm#

text
expected_progress = elapsed_time / total_flight_time
expected_delivery = total_goal * target_curve(expected_progress)
delivery_gap = expected_delivery - actual_delivery

if delivery_gap > threshold:
  increase priority / relax throttle / increase bid multiplier
if delivery_gap < -threshold:
  decrease priority / tighten throttle

Senior nuance：

加 smoothing，防止每分钟震荡。 · 对 live event 单独建 event-specific curve。 · 对 high-value guaranteed campaign 可以提高 delivery protection。 · 对 performance campaign 用 conversion/completion feedback 调整目标。

3.6 MLE layer#

supply forecast model：预测未来 eligible inventory。 · win rate model：预测某 line item 在当前 market 下赢的概率。 · performance model：预测 completion/conversion。 · counterfactual simulation：如果提高 bid/throttle，会带来多少 delivery/revenue。 · calibration：forecast error 要按 region/device/content/live/VOD 监控。

3.7 Failure modes#

event lag 导致 optimizer 以为没 delivery，突然放量 -> overdelivery。 · forecast 过高导致 underdelivery。 · creative not ready 导致 pacing 再激进也投不出去。 · targeting 太窄导致 eligible supply 不足。 · optimizer oscillation 造成服务不稳定。 · live event supply spike 没 override，错过 delivery window。

3.8 Metrics#

delivery ratio：actual / expected。 · spend smoothness。 · overdelivery/underdelivery rate。 · budget exhaustion timing。 · forecast error。 · win rate by segment。 · optimizer output churn。 · revenue/yield impact。

3.9 Senior answer template#

I would separate measurement from control. Events update delivered counters; forecast estimates future supply; optimizer periodically writes simple serving-time knobs such as throttle rate, priority score or bid multiplier. The ad server should not run heavy optimization in the request path. The hardest part is preventing feedback-loop instability, especially when event lag or forecast error makes the optimizer overreact.

4. 设计 live DAI for NFL-scale event#

4.1 问题本质#

Live DAI 难在 synchronized spike 和 playback continuity。VOD 可以 retry 和预取，Live event 的 ad break 是实时窗口，错过就错过。原则：

playback continuity > ad optimality

4.2 Requirements#

支持 NFL/WWE 这种 millions concurrent viewers。 · break signal 到 manifest 更新的 end-to-end latency 可控。 · ad pod duration 必须和 break duration 对齐。 · creative 必须预审、转码、CDN ready。 · fallback 必须不会黑屏。 · measurement 要能区分 ad decision、manifest、client playback。

4.3 Architecture#

text
Live Event Scheduler / SCTE Marker / Break Signal
  -> Break Orchestrator
  -> Ad Decision Prewarm
  -> Pod Assembly Service
       -> duration matching
       -> creative readiness
       -> frequency/category separation
  -> Manifest Generator
  -> Origin / CDN
  -> Client Playback
  -> Beacon/Event Pipeline

Control plane：

text
Event plan
  -> capacity reservation
  -> creative readiness matrix
  -> fallback slate plan
  -> regional warmup
  -> war-room dashboard

4.4 Senior design details#

precompute candidate pods before expected break when possible。 · ad decisioning can be per cohort, not always per user, when spike too high。 · manifest generator must scale horizontally and cache static segments。 · creative CDN readiness must check codec/device/duration/region availability。 · fallback slate should be pre-positioned everywhere。 · client SDK should handle tracking, timeout, fallback and resume content safely。

4.5 Latency budget#

text
break signal processing: 10-50 ms
ad decision / pod selection: 50-150 ms
manifest update: 50-200 ms
CDN/origin propagation: depends on architecture
client buffer/playback: must avoid stall

不要死背数字，重点是拆 budget，并说明哪里要 prewarm/cache。

4.6 MLE layer#

live 场景 ranking 要更保守，不能为了 marginal lift 冒 playback 风险。 · traffic spike 下可用 cohort-level decisioning 降低 per-user compute。 · forecasting 预测并发、break inventory、pod fill。 · anomaly detection 监控 black screen、beacon drop、manifest errors。

4.7 Failure modes / fallback#

FailureFallback
ad decision timeoutprecomputed pod / house ad
creative unavailablereplace with ready creative
manifest publish slowcontinue content or slate
CDN segment missslate / disable creative
beacon lagcontinue serving but mark measurement freshness degraded

4.8 Metrics#

break fill rate。 · ad decision latency。 · manifest publish latency。 · fallback slate rate。 · playback stall / black screen rate。 · CDN error rate。 · beacon success rate。 · pod duration mismatch rate。

4.9 Senior answer template#

I would design live DAI around pre-warming and graceful degradation. Before the event, I would reserve capacity, validate creatives, warm CDN paths and prepare fallback slates. During the event, the serving path should be short and observable. If decisioning or creative lookup is risky, I would prefer a safe fallback over breaking playback.

5. 设计 ads eventing pipeline#

5.1 问题本质#

Ads eventing 是整个 ads business 的账本。它驱动：

frequency cap。 · pacing/budget。 · reporting。 · measurement/attribution。 · billing/revenue recognition。 · ML training/optimization。

如果 event pipeline 不可信，ad server 再快也没用。

5.2 Requirements#

高吞吐、低延迟，同时支持 finalized correctness。 · schema governance。 · dedup/idempotency。 · late event / out-of-order event。 · privacy filtering。 · replay/backfill。 · downstream sink isolation。 · preliminary vs finalized metrics。

5.3 Architecture#

text
Client SDK / Server Ad Insertion / Partner Events
  -> Event Gateway
  -> Schema Validation
  -> Privacy Filter
  -> Kafka / Kinesis / Pulsar
  -> Stream Processor
       -> dedup
       -> enrichment
       -> watermark / late event handling
       -> quality checks
  -> Sinks
       -> frequency cap correction
       -> pacing aggregates
       -> reporting warehouse
       -> billing ledger
       -> ML feature/training store
       -> DLQ

5.4 Event contract#

text
AdsEvent:
  event_id
  request_id
  decision_id
  session_id
  member/profile/device scope
  campaign_id
  line_item_id
  creative_id
  event_type: request | impression | quartile | completion | click | conversion
  event_time
  ingest_time
  source
  schema_version
  privacy_flags

5.5 Correctness design#

event_id + source 做 dedup key。 · watermark 处理 late events。 · event validation 分 soft/hard failure。 · DLQ 按错误类型分流：schema、privacy、missing enrichment、sink failure。 · replay 必须 idempotent。 · billing/reporting 使用 finalized window。

5.6 MLE layer#

event pipeline 产出 ML training data。 · 需要 feature freshness、label quality、conversion delay handling。 · training data 要防止 leakage：不能把未来 event 放进 serving-time feature。 · 模型评估要知道 event attribution window 和 delayed labels。

5.7 Failure modes#

client beacon 丢失：server decision != actual impression。 · duplicate events：导致 overbilling 或 cap 误判。 · late conversions：影响 ROAS/reporting。 · schema deploy break：下游 pipeline 失败。 · partner event mismatch：第三方 measurement 和内部数据对不上。 · replay 不幂等：backfill 造成重复计费。

5.8 Metrics#

  • ingest QPS。
  • event lag p95/p99。
  • validation error rate。
  • dedup rate。
  • DLQ rate。
  • late event rate。
  • sink lag by downstream。
  • preliminary/finalized delta。
  • billing discrepancy rate。

5.9 Senior answer template#

I would design the eventing pipeline as both a streaming system and a financial correctness system. Real-time consumers need fast preliminary events, but reporting and billing need validated, deduplicated and finalized events. The key is explicit event contracts, idempotent replay, quality dashboards, and separate SLAs for serving feedback versus financial truth.

6. 设计 advertiser reporting API#

6.1 问题本质#

Advertiser reporting 不是普通 dashboard API。它是 advertiser trust、billing reconciliation、campaign optimization 的核心。难点：

metrics definition 必须统一。 · fresh data 和 final data 不一样。 · advertiser/agency 权限复杂。 · 查询维度多，数据量大。 · 和 billing/partner reports 可能不一致，需要 explanation。

6.2 Requirements#

支持 campaign/line item/creative/time/geo/device/content 等 slicing。 · dashboard 查询低延迟。 · 大报表 async export。 · access control / tenant isolation。 · freshness SLA。 · finalized window。 · metric lineage。 · audit。

6.3 Architecture#

text
Validated Ads Events
  -> Metrics Aggregation Jobs
       -> hourly/daily aggregates
       -> reach/frequency sketches
       -> conversion attribution tables
  -> Semantic Metrics Layer
  -> Reporting Query Service
       -> authz / tenant filter
       -> query planner
       -> cache / pre-aggregation
       -> pagination / async export
  -> API / UI / Partner Export

6.4 Core API#

text
POST /reports/query
Request:
  advertiser_id
  date_range
  dimensions: campaign, line_item, creative, geo, device
  metrics: impressions, completions, spend, reach, frequency, conversions
  filters
  freshness: latest | finalized

Response:
  rows
  totals
  last_updated_at
  finalized_until
  metric_definitions_version
  warnings

6.5 Data model#

fact_ad_event_validated：validated event-level data。 · agg_campaign_hourly：dashboard 快速查询。 · agg_reach_frequency：HLL/sketch 或 privacy-safe aggregate。 · metric_definition：统一 metrics 口径。 · report_job：async export 状态。 · access_grant：advertiser/agency/account 权限。

6.6 Senior trade-offs#

ProblemSolution
fresh vs correctexpose last_updated_at and finalized_until; preliminary data can change
flexible query vs latencypre-aggregate common dimensions; async export for expensive queries
reach/frequency exactnessuse sketches/aggregates, not user-level export
billing mismatchprovide reconciliation reason codes
partner API abuserate limit, quotas, cached reports

6.7 MLE / optimization connection#

Reporting feeds optimization:

advertiser uses reports to adjust targeting/budget/creative。 · ML uses aggregated outcome labels for conversion/completion models。 · reporting definitions must align with optimization labels，否则 model 优化的目标和客户看到的指标不一致。

6.8 Failure modes#

metric definition change causes apparent drop。 · late events change finalized numbers。 · agency sees unauthorized account。 · expensive query kills cluster。 · reporting and billing disagree。 · timezone/currency conversion error。

6.9 Metrics#

API p95 latency。 · cache hit rate。 · async report completion time。 · data freshness lag。 · query error rate。 · access denial/audit count。 · discrepancy with billing。 · top expensive dimensions。

6.10 Senior answer template#

I would not let every API query scan raw events. I would create a semantic metrics layer on top of validated events and pre-aggregated read models. The API should expose freshness and finalization metadata because ads reporting is not just fast analytics; it is a trust contract with advertisers.

7. 设计 clean room#

7.1 问题本质#

Clean room 是 privacy-safe collaboration。广告主想知道 overlap、conversion、incrementality，但平台不能泄露 user-level data。

核心原则：

No user-level export. Every query must be policy checked, aggregated, thresholded and audited.

7.2 Requirements#

partner onboarding。 · secure data ingestion。 · identity matching with privacy controls。 · approved query templates。 · aggregation threshold / suppression / noise。 · result export with policy。 · audit and compliance。

7.3 Architecture#

text
Partner Data Upload
  -> encrypted landing zone
  -> schema validation
  -> identity normalization / hashing
  -> isolated clean room storage

neo Ads Data
  -> privacy-safe projection
  -> clean room join keys

Query Service
  -> template registry
  -> policy engine
  -> secure compute
  -> aggregation threshold
  -> result release
  -> audit log

7.4 Data model#

text
PartnerDataset:
  partner_id
  dataset_id
  schema_version
  data_version
  allowed_purpose
  retention_policy

CleanRoomQuery:
  query_id
  template_id
  requester
  purpose
  input_datasets
  policy_version
  status

ResultRelease:
  query_id
  aggregation_level
  minimum_cohort_size
  suppression_applied
  noise_applied
  approved_at

7.5 Supported use cases#

audience overlap。 · conversion measurement。 · incrementality analysis。 · reach/frequency by aggregated segment。 · campaign performance by privacy-safe cohort。

7.6 Senior trade-offs#

ProblemSenior answer
utility vs privacyuse templates, thresholds, purpose limitation, aggregation
custom SQL vs safetyallow constrained templates first; advanced queries require review
identity match qualityexpose match rate and confidence; do not overstate precision
repeated queries leaking dataquery budget, differential privacy/noise, audit
partner trustdeterministic policy and explainable result suppression

7.7 MLE layer#

  • clean room outputs can train/validate audience or conversion models only in aggregated/privacy-safe way。
  • incrementality models need holdouts and bias controls。
  • identity match error affects labels; model evaluation must account for match coverage。

7.8 Failure modes#

user-level data accidentally exported。 · small cohort leakage。 · partner uploads wrong schema。 · join key mismatch gives misleading low match rate。 · repeated slicing reconstructs sensitive info。 · result not reproducible because data version changed。

7.9 Metrics#

match rate。 · query approval time。 · suppressed result rate。 · policy violation blocked。 · result export count。 · audit completeness。 · partner dataset freshness。

7.10 Senior answer template#

I would design clean room as a policy-controlled compute platform, not a flexible data warehouse. The value is privacy-safe measurement, so the system must constrain data movement, query templates, aggregation thresholds, result release and audit. If a query is powerful enough to leak user-level data, it should not be self-serve.

8. 设计 creative approval and distribution system#

8.1 问题本质#

Creative 系统连接 advertiser workflow 和 serving safety。广告素材如果没有正确 approval/transcode/CDN readiness，最后会变成播放错误、brand safety 问题或 revenue loss。

8.2 Requirements#

upload + metadata validation。 · transcode to device/CTV-compatible formats。 · brand safety / policy scan。 · human review + approval workflow。 · preview exactly like serving environment。 · CDN distribution and readiness check。 · serving index only includes ready creatives。 · support GenAI variants / modular creative。

8.3 Architecture#

text
Creative Upload API
  -> Metadata Validator
  -> Media Storage
  -> Transcode Pipeline
  -> Automated QA
       -> duration
       -> codec
       -> loudness
       -> black frame
       -> brand safety
  -> Human Review Queue
  -> Approval State Store
  -> CDN Distribution
  -> Readiness Checker
  -> Serving Creative Index

8.4 Data model#

text
Creative:
  creative_id
  advertiser_id
  campaign_id
  asset_uri
  duration_ms
  format
  approval_status
  policy_scan_status
  transcode_status
  cdn_status
  compatibility_matrix
  version

CreativeVariant:
  parent_creative_id
  variant_id
  source: uploaded | generated
  prompt/model_version
  brand_kit_id
  approval_status

8.5 Senior design details#

serving path 不实时检查大文件，只读 creative readiness index。 · creative state machine 必须防止半成品进入 serving。 · preview URL 要和真实 CDN/encoding 尽量一致。 · programmatic creative 需要 pre-approval cache。 · GenAI variants 增加 cardinality，需要 automated policy/QA，否则人工审核被打爆。

8.6 MLE / GenAI layer#

brand safety classifier。 · OCR/speech-to-text 检测敏感内容。 · creative quality scoring。 · modular creative ranking。 · GenAI prompt/model lineage。 · human review feedback 回流模型。

8.7 Failure modes#

creative approved but CDN not ready。 · CTV codec incompatible 导致黑屏。 · duration mismatch 导致 ad pod break。 · programmatic creative 未预审。 · GenAI variant 缺 brand compliance。 · reviewer decision inconsistent。

8.8 Metrics#

time-to-approval。 · transcode failure rate。 · policy reject reason distribution。 · CDN readiness lag。 · creative serving error rate。 · device compatibility failure。 · GenAI variant approval rate。 · reviewer SLA。

8.9 Senior answer template#

I would treat creative readiness as a serving-critical state machine. The ad server should never discover at request time that a creative is invalid. Upload, QA, review, transcode, CDN readiness and serving-index publication must be explicit, observable and versioned.

9. 设计 forecasting and simulation platform#

9.1 问题本质#

Forecasting / simulation 是 sales、planning、pricing、pacing 的前置系统。它回答：

有多少可卖 inventory？ · 这个 audience/targeting 能不能交付？ · 价格/预算/flight 改变会怎样？ · live schedule 或 content mix 改变会怎样？

9.2 Requirements#

forecast availability by targeting。 · estimate reach/frequency。 · predict delivery risk。 · simulate booking scenarios。 · support pricing/yield planning。 · versioned, reproducible snapshots。 · confidence intervals and explanation。

9.3 Architecture#

text
Historical Ads Events
  -> Inventory Feature Builder
Content / Live Schedule
  -> Supply Forecast Model
Current Bookings / Campaigns
  -> Demand Reservation Model
Audience / Targeting Store
  -> Eligibility Estimator

Forecast Service
  -> availability API
  -> delivery risk API
  -> reach/frequency estimator

Simulation Platform
  -> scenario config
  -> traffic replay / synthetic supply
  -> allocation optimizer
  -> results + explanation

9.4 Core API#

text
POST /forecast
Request:
  targeting_spec
  flight
  geo/device/content constraints
  budget_or_goal
  scenario_overrides

Response:
  available_impressions
  expected_reach
  expected_frequency
  delivery_risk
  confidence_interval
  limiting_factors
  forecast_snapshot_id

9.5 MLE layer#

time-series supply forecast。 · seasonality / content release / live event features。 · audience eligibility model。 · delivery risk classifier。 · reach/frequency estimator。 · scenario simulation with counterfactual assumptions。

Senior nuance：

forecast 不是精确承诺，要输出 confidence interval。 · replay simulation 有 counterfactual bias，因为过去没有发生的 auction 不可完全观察。 · live events 要单独建模型，不能用普通 VOD 平均流量。 · forecast error 要回流校准 pacing optimizer。

9.6 Data model#

text
ForecastSnapshot:
  snapshot_id
  data_cutoff_time
  model_version
  supply_version
  booking_version
  assumptions

ForecastResult:
  snapshot_id
  targeting_hash
  flight
  available_impressions
  confidence_interval
  limiting_factors

9.7 Failure modes#

forecast overestimates supply -> campaign underdelivery。 · content schedule changes not reflected。 · targeting segment stale。 · current bookings missing。 · model ignores live spike。 · simulation result not reproducible。

9.8 Metrics#

forecast MAPE / calibration error。 · delivery risk precision/recall。 · forecast freshness。 · scenario runtime。 · sales plan override rate。 · underdelivery due to forecast miss。 · limiting factor explanation coverage。

9.9 Senior answer template#

I would make forecasts versioned products, not ephemeral calculations. Every media plan should reference a forecast snapshot with data cutoff, model version, supply assumptions and confidence. Then if a campaign underdelivers, we can explain whether the miss came from forecast error, targeting changes, creative readiness or serving/pacing behavior.

10. 设计 sales/ad ops agent#

10.1 问题本质#

Sales/ad ops agent 不是聊天机器人。它是 ads workflow automation layer，帮助人查 campaign、解释 forecast/reporting、发现配置风险、生成 proposal、triage underdelivery，但不能绕过业务控制。

10.2 Requirements#

RAG over ads docs、contracts、campaign configs、reports、policies。 · tool calling：forecast、reporting、campaign validation、creative readiness、ticket creation。 · permission-aware。 · write operation approval。 · audit trace。 · reliable citations。 · evaluation for workflow correctness。

10.3 Architecture#

text
User Request
  -> Agent Gateway
  -> Intent Classifier
  -> Permission Engine
  -> Retrieval Layer
       -> ads knowledge graph
       -> vector search over docs/policies/reports
       -> entity lookup
  -> Planner
  -> Tool Router
       -> Forecast API
       -> Reporting API
       -> Campaign API
       -> Creative Readiness API
       -> Ticket/Workflow API
  -> Approval Workflow
  -> Response with citations + action trace

10.4 Data / knowledge model#

  advertiser -> account -> campaign -> line_item -> creative
  campaign -> report -> invoice -> measurement study
  campaign -> forecast_snapshot -> delivery_risk
  creative -> approval_status -> CDN_status
