10.6 Safety / governance#

read-only by default。 · destructive/write operations require approval。 · every answer cites source objects。 · no user-level data in prompt/context。 · permission scope included in cache/retrieval key。 · tool outputs structured, not free-form。 · audit log for compliance。

10.7 MLE / AI evaluation#

RAG retrieval quality：source hit rate、citation correctness。 · task success：issue detection、time saved、ad ops acceptance。 · hallucination rate。 · unsafe action blocked。 · regression suite with real workflow scenarios。 · human feedback loop。

10.8 Failure modes#

agent cites stale campaign config。 · incorrect permission exposes account data。 · tool output misunderstood by LLM。 · agent recommends budget change without forecast evidence。 · RAG retrieves policy for wrong region。 · action replay duplicates campaign update。

10.9 Metrics#

adoption。 · task completion rate。 · human acceptance rate。 · average handling time reduction。 · issue detection precision/recall。 · citation accuracy。 · approval rejection rate。 · unsafe action attempts blocked。

10.10 Senior answer template#

I would position the agent as a workflow assistant with strict tool boundaries. It can retrieve, explain, validate and draft actions, but production changes go through typed APIs, permission checks, idempotency keys and human approval. The system should be evaluated like a production workflow, not like a chatbot demo.

11. Implementation Templates：10 个 ads system design 怎么落地#

这一节是最后 3 分钟补强。如果面试官追问 “具体怎么实现？” 就从这里拿细节。

Global ad server implementation#

Core API：POST /adDecision 输入 member/context/content/ad_break/privacy flags，输出 selected pod、creative URLs、tracking metadata、decision reasons。Serving path 必须 stateless-ish，依赖 state 走低延迟 cache/store。

关键实现：

  • Campaign serving index 按 geo、device、content category、flight、format、deal/priority 做 coarse partition。
  • Frequency cap、pacing、creative readiness 都是 serving-critical lookup，要有 timeout 和 fallback。
  • Decision log 要记录 rejected reason，不然 fill rate/underdelivery debug 会非常痛苦。
  • Regional failover 要处理 duplicate exposure 和 event reconciliation。
  • ML scoring 要有 model_version、feature_version、timeout fallback 和 online/offline metric 对齐。

Frequency capping implementation#

Data model：cap_key = identity_scope + campaign/advertiser/creative + window。Value 存 count、last_seen、source、TTL。Identity scope 可以是 profile、account、household、device，confidence 要显式传给 downstream。

关键实现：

Strong consistency 太贵时，用 local cache + fast remote store + validated event correction。 · Client beacon 丢失会导致 undercount；server-selected ad 不等于真实 impression。 · Cap hierarchy：creative cap、campaign cap、advertiser cap、category cap。 · Over-cap dashboard 按 device/region/identity source 分解。 · 对严格 cap 可以 selective stronger consistency；不要把所有请求都放进 global lock。

Pacing and budget optimizer implementation#

Data model：line item 有 goal、budget、flight、delivered、remaining、forecast supply、win rate、priority。Optimizer 周期性输出 throttle_rate、bid_multiplier、priority_score 到 serving cache。

关键实现：

  • Streaming aggregation 更新 delivered counts，batch job 做 final correction。
  • 防 oscillation：pacing 调整要有 smoothing，不要每分钟剧烈改变。
  • Live events 需要 event-specific override，因为 supply spike 集中。
  • Underdelivery alert 要能解释：targeting too narrow、forecast wrong、creative not ready、bid too low、pacing too strict。
  • optimizer 输出要 versioned，方便 rollback 和 A/B。

Live DAI implementation#

Critical path：break signal -> ad decision -> pod assembly -> manifest update -> CDN/origin publish -> client playback -> beacon events。Live 场景的核心是 synchronized spike 和不可重来。

关键实现：

  • Event 前预热 ad server、manifest generator、creative CDN、caches。
  • Creative readiness matrix：codec、duration、device compatibility、CDN availability。
  • Break-level dashboard：signal time、decision latency、manifest publish latency、fallback slate rate、playback error。
  • Fallback 优先级：precomputed pod -> house ad -> slate -> continue content。原则是 playback continuity > ad optimality。
  • cohort-level decisioning 可以在 spike 下保护系统，不必所有场景都 per-user real-time ranking。

Ads eventing implementation#

Event contract：每个 event 必须有 event_id、request_id、session_id、ad_id、campaign_id、event_type、event_time、ingest_time、source、schema_version。

关键实现：

Watermark + allowed lateness 支持 late events。 · DLQ 分 validation error、policy error、enrichment missing、sink unavailable。 · Replay 必须 idempotent，不然 backfill 会重复计费。 · Billing/reporting 要有 preliminary vs finalized 数据窗口。 · ML training labels 要记录 attribution window，避免 label leakage。

Advertiser reporting API implementation#

Read model：用 validated events 生成 pre-aggregated metrics，按 campaign/line item/creative/time/geo/device 维度查询。复杂报告走 async export，轻量 dashboard 走 cached query。

关键实现：

Semantic metric layer 统一 impression、completion、reach、frequency、spend、conversion 定义。 · Report access control：advertiser 只能看自己的 campaign，agency 只能看授权账户。 · 数据新鲜度要显示：last_updated_at、finalized_until。 · 与 billing 不一致时，要有 reconciliation explanation。 · semantic layer 版本必须记录，否则 metric 改名/改定义会让客户觉得数据坏了。

Clean room implementation#

Compute model：partner 数据进入 isolated encrypted zone，approved query templates 才能运行。结果必须经过 aggregation threshold、suppression/noise、policy release。

关键实现：

不允许 user-level row export。 · Query policy 要检查 purpose、columns、join keys、minimum cohort size。 · 每次结果带 policy_version、data_version、threshold。 · Audit log 记录 query text/template、requester、approval、result shape。 · repeated slicing 可能泄露数据，所以要 query budget / suppression。

Creative approval and distribution implementation#

Workflow：upload -> scan -> transcode -> policy/brand safety -> preview -> reviewer approval -> CDN readiness -> serving index update。

关键实现：

Serving index 只包含 approved + ready creatives。 · Programmatic creative 要有 pre-approval cache，实时路径不临时下载大素材。 · GenAI creative variants 要记录 parent creative、prompt/model、brand kit、approval decision。 · Device compatibility tests 防止 CTV 黑屏、音量异常、duration mismatch。 · creative state machine 必须保证 approved 和 ready_to_serve 是两个不同状态。

Forecasting and simulation implementation#

Inputs：historical delivery、content/live schedule、current bookings、inventory constraints、seasonality、targeting availability。Outputs：availability、reach/frequency、delivery risk、yield scenario。

关键实现：

Forecast snapshot versioned，media plan 引用 snapshot id。 · Simulation 用 historical traffic replay，但要声明 counterfactual bias。 · Sales/ad ops 需要 explainability：为什么某个 campaign 有 underdelivery risk。 · Online feedback 校准 forecast error，按 region/device/content/live/VOD 分段。 · confidence interval 是必须的；不要把 forecast 当确定承诺。

Sales/ad ops agent implementation#

Architecture：LLM planner + ads entity graph + RAG + tool registry + permission engine + approval workflow。Agent 的强项是解释和辅助配置，不是绕过业务控制。

关键实现：

  • Tool output 必须 structured，不能让 LLM 自由编 campaign fields。
  • Write operation 需要 approval 和 idempotency key。
  • RAG citation 必须能跳回 source object：contract、forecast、campaign、report、policy。
  • Eval scenarios 覆盖 proposal draft、forecast explanation、underdelivery diagnosis、creative readiness triage。
  • Agent trace 要记录 retrieved sources、tool inputs/outputs、approval 和 final action。

Debug / Incident 高频场景#

1. Fill rate 突然下降怎么查？#

先按 region/device/content/ad format/live/VOD 分段。看 supply eligibility、campaign index freshness、targeting filter、frequency cap、pacing throttles、creative readiness、programmatic timeouts、policy changes。再看最近 deploy/config/model change。止血可以 rollback、relax noncritical filter、use fallback demand、disable broken partner。长期加 reason-code dashboard。

2. 某 campaign underdelivery 怎么查？#

看 forecast 是否过高、targeting 是否太窄、budget/pacing 是否过严、creative 是否未 ready、frequency cap 是否限制、auction win rate 是否低、inventory split 是否变化。比较 eligible impressions vs served impressions。输出给 ad ops 的解释要可行动：扩大 targeting、提高 bid、换 creative、延长 flight。

3. 用户看到重复广告怎么查？#

看 frequency cap read/write、identity resolution、exposure event latency、client beacon loss、dedup、pod diversity rules、fallback pod。可能是 server selected 不重复但 client retry 播放重复，也可能是 identity merge 延迟。要用 request_id/session_id/ad_id 串 trace。修复可能在 cap store、eventing、podding、client integration。

4. Live event 播放中广告黑屏怎么查？#

先确认是否 creative encoding/CDN/manifest issue。看 manifest update、segment availability、creative duration alignment、client playback errors、device-specific codec。止血使用 slate/fallback 或 disable problematic creative。长期加 pre-event creative readiness check、device matrix tests、manifest validation。

5. Reporting 数字和 invoice 不一致怎么查？#

看 metric definition、event finalization window、dedup version、late events、pricing terms、adjustments、timezone、currency。Reporting 和 billing 可能使用不同 cutoffs。需要 reconciliation table 解释差异。修复方向是 shared semantic layer 或明确 preliminary/final numbers。

6. Programmatic revenue suddenly drops 怎么查？#

看 bid request volume、DSP timeout、bid rate、win rate、floor price、creative reject、deal config、privacy signal changes、partner outage。按 partner/deal/region/device 分解。止血可能是降低 floor、disable bad partner, route more direct demand, rollback request schema change。

7. Targeting segment size 异常怎么查？#

看 audience ingestion、identity match rate、schema validation、lookback window、privacy thresholds、data freshness、segment computation job。Advertiser uploaded data 格式变化会导致 match rate 下降。需要 status API 告诉 advertiser 问题在哪里，而不是 silently producing tiny audience。

8. Event pipeline lag 怎么查？#

看 producer volume、broker throughput、consumer lag、schema validation errors、DLQ growth、external sink slowness、hot partitions。Ads pipeline 要优先保障实时 decisioning-critical streams，再处理 reporting batch。长期要做 backpressure、partitioning、autoscaling、replay tooling。

ML / Optimization 面试重点#

这一节要讲成 Ads MLE system design，不是只说 “train a model”。Senior MLE 面试会追问：training data 从哪里来、label 怎么定义、offline feature 和 online feature 如何一致、PyTorch model 怎么写、model 如何上线、latency 怎么控、delayed conversion / selection bias / counterfactual 怎么处理、如何监控模型是不是伤害了 member experience。

0. Ads ML End-to-End 架构#

text
Ad Serving / Client / Partner Events
  -> Kafka / Kinesis / Pulsar
  -> Flink / Spark Streaming
       -> validation
       -> dedup
       -> sessionization
       -> attribution window
       -> online counters
  -> S3 / Iceberg / Delta Lake
       -> raw_events
       -> validated_events
       -> training_examples
       -> labels

Feature Pipelines
  -> Spark / Flink / dbt
  -> Offline Feature Store
       (S3/Iceberg, Hive, Snowflake, BigQuery)
  -> Online Feature Store
       (Redis, DynamoDB, Cassandra, Feast online store)

PyTorch Training
  -> Dataset / DataLoader
  -> ranking / multi-task model
  -> calibration
  -> offline eval
  -> model registry

Serving
  -> export TorchScript / ONNX
  -> Triton / Ray Serve / KServe
  -> ad decisioning service
  -> shadow / canary / A/B
  -> online metrics + drift monitoring

一句高级回答：

I would design ads ML as a closed loop: events create labels, feature pipelines create point-in-time correct training data, PyTorch trains ranking/outcome models, the model is exported to a low-latency serving runtime, and online decisions feed back into measurement, calibration and retraining. The hard parts are not only model architecture, but label correctness, feature freshness, delayed outcomes, selection bias, serving latency and safe experimentation.

1. Ads ranking model 输入特征有哪些？#

1.1 Feature groups#

Feature groupExamplesOnline source
member/contextprofile/cohort, geo, device, app version, time of day, household signalsidentity/context service, Redis cache
contenttitle id, genre, maturity rating, live/VOD, language, content contextcontent metadata service
ad/campaignadvertiser, campaign objective, line item, bid/floor, flight, pacing pressurecampaign serving index
creativeduration, format, category, historical VCR, quality score, approval statecreative readiness store
frequency/recencylast exposure, exposures in window, advertiser/category fatigueRedis/DynamoDB cap store
marketplaceeligible count, partner latency, deal type, reserve/floor, win rateserving features
historical performanceCTR/VCR/conversion/reach by segmentoffline/online feature store
policy/brand safetyprivacy flags, sensitive content labels, category blockspolicy engine

1.2 Labels / objectives#

CTV/streaming ads 不一定主要优化 click：

pCTR：click probability，web/mobile 有用，CTV 弱一些。 · pVCR：video completion rate / quartile completion。 · pConversion：conversion probability，delay 长。 · pNegativeFeedback：hide/report/complaint/proxy。 · brand lift proxy：通常更难，需要 study 或 aggregate measurement。 · revenue / yield：bid * predicted outcome。 · member experience：ad fatigue、repeat rate、playback interruption。

1.3 Training example#

text
TrainingExample:
  request_id
  candidate_ad_id
  member/context features
  content features
  campaign/ad/creative features
  serving-time pacing/cap features
  was_selected
  impression_label
  completion_label
  click_label
  conversion_label
  event_time
  attribution_window
  sample_weight

关键：

  • 训练样本必须是 point-in-time correct，不能用未来信息。
  • negative samples 要小心：未展示广告没有真实 outcome，只能作为 candidate/ranking negative，不是 conversion negative。
  • selected-only data 有 selection bias，需要 exploration、IPS 或 simulation。

1.4 PyTorch ranking model solution#

面试中可以讲一个 Wide & Deep / DLRM-like multi-task ranking model：

categorical features 走 embedding。 · dense features 走 MLP。 · multi-task heads 同时预测 pCTR、pVCR、pCVR、pNegativeFeedback。 · 最终 serving score 由 business ranker 组合，不是直接拿一个 head 排序。

python
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

class AdsRankingModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, vocab_sizes, dense_dim, emb_dim=32, hidden_dim=256):
        super().__init__()
        self.embeddings = nn.ModuleDict({
            name: nn.Embedding(vocab_size, emb_dim)
            for name, vocab_size in vocab_sizes.items()
        })
        sparse_dim = len(vocab_sizes) * emb_dim
        input_dim = sparse_dim + dense_dim

self.backbone = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.BatchNorm1d(hidden_dim),
            nn.Dropout(0.1),
            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, hidden_dim),
            nn.ReLU(),
        )
        self.ctr_head = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 1)
        self.vcr_head = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 1)
        self.cvr_head = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 1)
        self.negative_head = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 1)

def forward(self, sparse_features, dense_features):
        emb_list = []
        for name, emb in self.embeddings.items():
            emb_list.append(emb(sparse_features[name]))
        sparse_vec = torch.cat(emb_list, dim=-1)
        x = torch.cat([sparse_vec, dense_features], dim=-1)
        h = self.backbone(x)
        return {
            "ctr_logit": self.ctr_head(h).squeeze(-1),
            "vcr_logit": self.vcr_head(h).squeeze(-1),
            "cvr_logit": self.cvr_head(h).squeeze(-1),
            "negative_logit": self.negative_head(h).squeeze(-1),
        }

Loss 要处理 multi-task 和 delayed label：

python
def ads_multitask_loss(outputs, batch):
    loss_ctr = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
        outputs["ctr_logit"],
        batch["clicked"].float(),
        weight=batch["sample_weight"],
    )
    loss_vcr = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
        outputs["vcr_logit"],
        batch["completed"].float(),
        weight=batch["sample_weight"],
    )

valid_cvr = batch["conversion_label_ready"].float()
    loss_cvr_raw = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
        outputs["cvr_logit"],
        batch["converted"].float(),
        reduction="none",
    )
    loss_cvr = (loss_cvr_raw * valid_cvr * batch["sample_weight"]).sum() / valid_cvr.sum().clamp_min(1.0)

loss_neg = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
        outputs["negative_logit"],
        batch["negative_feedback"].float(),
        weight=batch["sample_weight"],
    )
    return loss_ctr + 0.8 * loss_vcr + 1.5 * loss_cvr + 1.2 * loss_neg

Training loop：

python
model = AdsRankingModel(vocab_sizes=vocab_sizes, dense_dim=dense_dim)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=1e-5)

for epoch in range(num_epochs):
    model.train()
    for batch in train_loader:
        outputs = model(batch["sparse"], batch["dense"])
        loss = ads_multitask_loss(outputs, batch)

optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=5.0)
        optimizer.step()

model.eval()
    # Evaluate AUC, logloss, calibration, NDCG@k, VCR lift, revenue/yield proxy.

Serving score 不能只看 pCTR：

python
def compute_serving_score(pred, bid, pacing_pressure, fatigue_penalty, quality_penalty):
    p_ctr = torch.sigmoid(pred["ctr_logit"])
    p_vcr = torch.sigmoid(pred["vcr_logit"])
    p_cvr = torch.sigmoid(pred["cvr_logit"])
    p_negative = torch.sigmoid(pred["negative_logit"])

value = 0.2 * p_ctr + 0.4 * p_vcr + 0.8 * p_cvr
    business_value = bid * value * pacing_pressure
    member_risk = 2.0 * p_negative + fatigue_penalty + quality_penalty
    return business_value - member_risk

面试解释：

The PyTorch model predicts multiple outcomes, but the production ranker applies business constraints and penalties. Privacy, policy, frequency cap and creative readiness are hard filters; the model only ranks eligible candidates.

2. Ads ML serving 有什么特殊要求？#

2.1 Serving architecture#

text
Ad Decisioning Service
  -> Candidate Retrieval
  -> Feature Fetch
       -> Redis / Cassandra / DynamoDB online feature store
       -> request context features
       -> campaign/creative features
  -> Model Scoring Service
       -> Triton / Ray Serve / KServe
       -> PyTorch TorchScript / ONNX model
       -> batch candidates per request
  -> Business Ranker
       -> constraints
       -> pacing / bid / fatigue penalties
  -> Decision response

2.2 PyTorch export / production serving#

python
model.eval()
example_sparse = {
    name: torch.zeros(8, dtype=torch.long)
    for name in vocab_sizes.keys()
}
example_dense = torch.zeros(8, dense_dim)

traced = torch.jit.trace(model, (example_sparse, example_dense), strict=False)
traced.save("ads_ranking_model.pt")

生产中可以选：

TorchScript：直接服务 PyTorch graph。 · ONNX：给 Triton / ONNX Runtime 做优化。 · Triton：高吞吐、多模型、多框架。 · Ray Serve：Python-heavy feature transform / ensemble / dynamic routing。 · KServe：Kubernetes-native rollout。

2.3 Latency / fallback#

  • 一次 ad request 会有多个 candidates，模型要 batch score。
  • feature fetch 要 batch get。
  • model timeout 必须 fallback 到 heuristic/cached score。
  • 模型不应该直接调用慢 warehouse/S3。
  • online features 要有 freshness timestamp。
  • serving response 写 model_version、feature_version、calibration_version、fallback_reason。

2.4 Monitoring#

model p50/p95/p99 latency。 · timeout/fallback rate。 · feature missing rate。 · score distribution drift。 · calibration drift。 · prediction-business metric correlation。 · pCTR/pVCR/pCVR head health。 · revenue/fill/member QoE guardrails。

回答模板：

Ads ML serving is a real-time ranking dependency, so I would treat it like critical infrastructure. The model must batch-score candidates, respect strict timeouts, expose model/feature versions, and degrade to heuristic scoring if the model or feature store is unhealthy.

3. Multi-objective optimization 怎么讲？#

Multi-objective 不要说成简单 weighted sum。先分清楚：

Hard constraints：

privacy / consent。 · policy / brand safety。 · creative readiness。 · frequency cap。 · legal/contractual restrictions。

Soft objectives：

revenue/yield。 · advertiser outcome。 · campaign delivery。 · member experience。 · creative quality。 · exploration。

text
Candidates
  -> hard filters
  -> ML predicted outcomes
  -> calibrated scores
  -> business score
  -> guardrail constraints
  -> auction/podding

Score example：

text
score =
  bid * calibrated_p_outcome
  * pacing_multiplier
  * quality_multiplier
  - fatigue_penalty
  - negative_feedback_penalty

Senior nuance：

不是所有约束都能变成权重。privacy 和 brand safety 是 hard constraint。 · 权重需要通过 offline simulation + A/B test 调。 · 不同 campaign objective 使用不同 head 或不同 calibration。 · 需要 guardrails：repeat ad rate、playback error、complaints、under/overdelivery。

3.1 Calibration 为什么一定要提#

Ads 系统里 raw model score 不一定可比较：

不同 device/geo/content 的 score 分布不同。 · pCTR/pCVR 可能 miscalibrated。 · bid * probability 如果 probability 不准，会伤 auction/yield。

做法：

Platt scaling / isotonic regression。 · segment-level calibration by region/device/content。 · calibration dashboard：predicted vs actual。 · model registry 保存 calibration version。 · online ranker 使用 calibrated score。

4. Traffic shaping models 是什么？#

Traffic shaping 是控制流量去向：

哪些 requests 给 programmatic。 · 哪些 supply 给 guaranteed campaign。 · 哪些 partner 被 fanout。 · 哪些请求使用 expensive model。 · live spike 时哪些 personalization 被降级。

text
Request stream
  -> traffic shaping policy/model
       -> direct only
       -> direct + programmatic fanout
       -> cohort-level decision
       -> expensive model scoring
       -> cheap heuristic scoring
  -> ad decisioning

Features：

request context。 · campaign delivery pressure。 · partner latency/win rate。 · forecasted supply。 · model cost/latency。 · live/VOD state。

Metrics：

fill/revenue。 · partner timeout。 · model serving cost。 · p99 latency。 · guaranteed delivery risk。 · member QoE。

Senior answer：

I would not send every request to every partner or every model. Traffic shaping is a control layer that decides which demand paths are worth the latency and cost for this request, given pacing pressure, partner health, yield and playback constraints.

5. Outcome-based optimization 怎么做？#

Outcome-based optimization 要从 impression optimization 升级到 business goal optimization。

Goals：

conversion。 · video completion。 · reach/frequency。 · brand lift。 · incremental outcome。 · advertiser ROAS。

Pipeline：

text
Impression / exposure
  -> attribution window
  -> conversion / completion / brand outcome events
  -> label builder
  -> delayed label handling
  -> PyTorch outcome model
  -> calibrated score
  -> online optimization

难点：

delayed labels：conversion 可能几小时/几天后到。 · selection bias：只知道展示过广告的 outcome。 · attribution bias：last-touch 不一定 causal。 · privacy：outcome signals 可能只能 aggregate / clean room。 · cold start：新 advertiser/creative 没历史。

方法：

attribution window and label readiness。 · exploration traffic。 · inverse propensity scoring IPS。 · doubly robust estimator。 · uplift / incrementality model。 · holdout groups。 · clean-room aggregate measurement。

面试模板：

I would be careful not to treat observed conversions as unbiased labels. Ads outcomes are delayed and selected. I would use attribution windows, label-readiness flags, exploration traffic, and experiment-based validation before trusting an outcome model in production.

6. Offline simulation 和 A/B test 如何结合？#

上线流程：

text
Offline training
  -> offline metrics
       AUC / logloss / calibration / NDCG@k
  -> offline replay simulation
       fill, revenue proxy, delivery, guardrails
  -> shadow serving
       latency, score distribution, feature missing
  -> canary
       small traffic, no major business impact
  -> A/B test
       true member + advertiser outcomes
  -> ramp
       guardrail monitoring

Offline simulation 能测：

ranking changes。 · pacing/yield changes。 · partner fanout policy。 · fill and delivery risk。 · latency/cost estimate。

但 simulation 有 limitations：

historical logs 没有未展示广告的真实 outcome。 · partner bids may change if traffic changes。 · member behavior may change。 · delayed conversion cannot be fully known in short window。

必须用 A/B：

true causal effect。 · member QoE。 · advertiser outcome。 · marketplace equilibrium。 · long-term delivery/revenue impact。

7. ML Incident / Debug Playbook#

7.1 Model score suddenly shifts#

排查：

model version changed? · feature version changed? · feature missing rate? · categorical id mapping drift? · calibration version changed? · training data distribution shift? · online/offline feature mismatch?

止血：

rollback model。 · rollback feature config。 · disable new feature。 · fallback to previous ranker。

7.2 Revenue up but member complaints up#

说明只优化 revenue 可能伤 experience。看：

repeat ad rate。 · negative feedback。 · completion drop。 · ad category fatigue。 · p99 latency / playback error。 · creative quality。

7.3 Conversion model looks good offline but fails online#

可能原因：

selection bias。 · label leakage。 · delayed labels not mature。 · attribution mismatch。 · calibration bad。 · experiment population different。

7.4 Feature store lag#

影响：

pacing/frequency features stale。 · conversion/recent exposure features wrong。 · model score drift。

需要：

feature freshness timestamp。 · stale-feature fallback。 · monitoring by feature group。 · point-in-time offline join。

8. PyTorch 面试短答模板#

如果面试官问 “How would you implement the ranking model in PyTorch?”：

I would start with a DLRM/Wide-and-Deep style PyTorch model: embeddings for sparse categorical features such as advertiser, campaign, creative, content and device; dense features for pacing, frequency, bid, recency and historical performance; a shared MLP backbone; and multi-task heads for pCTR, pVCR, pCVR and negative feedback. I would train with BCE losses, sample weights, label-readiness masks for delayed conversion, and then calibrate the heads before serving. The production serving score would combine calibrated outcome probabilities with bid, pacing pressure and member-experience penalties, while policy/privacy/frequency cap remain hard filters.

如果问 “How does it enter production?”：

I would export the trained PyTorch model to TorchScript or ONNX, serve it through Triton/Ray Serve/KServe, batch-score candidates per ad request, enforce strict timeout, and fallback to cached or heuristic scoring. Every decision log should include model version, feature version, calibration version, and score breakdown so we can debug business changes.

9. Python Dataset / DataLoader：训练数据怎么喂给 PyTorch？#

面试官如果追问 “How do you build training data?”，不要只说 read parquet。要说 point-in-time features、label readiness、sample weight、sparse/dense split。

python
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Dict, List

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader

@dataclass
class FeatureSpec:
    sparse_names: List[str]
    dense_names: List[str]
    label_names: List[str]

class AdsRankingDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, rows, spec: FeatureSpec):
        self.rows = rows
        self.spec = spec

def __len__(self):
        return len(self.rows)

def __getitem__(self, idx):
        row = self.rows[idx]
        sparse = {
            name: int(row.get(name, 0))
            for name in self.spec.sparse_names
        }
        dense = np.array(
            [float(row.get(name, 0.0)) for name in self.spec.dense_names],
            dtype=np.float32,
        )
        labels = {
            name: float(row.get(name, 0.0))
            for name in self.spec.label_names
        }
        return {
            "sparse": sparse,
            "dense": dense,
            "labels": labels,
            "sample_weight": float(row.get("sample_weight", 1.0)),
            "conversion_label_ready": float(row.get("conversion_label_ready", 0.0)),
        }

def collate_ads_batch(batch):
    sparse_names = batch[0]["sparse"].keys()
    label_names = batch[0]["labels"].keys()
    return {
        "sparse": {
            name: torch.tensor([x["sparse"][name] for x in batch], dtype=torch.long)
            for name in sparse_names
        },
        "dense": torch.tensor(np.stack([x["dense"] for x in batch]), dtype=torch.float32),
        "sample_weight": torch.tensor([x["sample_weight"] for x in batch], dtype=torch.float32),
        "conversion_label_ready": torch.tensor(
            [x["conversion_label_ready"] for x in batch],
            dtype=torch.float32,
        ),
        **{
            name: torch.tensor([x["labels"][name] for x in batch], dtype=torch.float32)
            for name in label_names
        },
    }

loader = DataLoader(
    AdsRankingDataset(training_rows, feature_spec),
    batch_size=1024,
    shuffle=True,
    num_workers=4,
    collate_fn=collate_ads_batch,
)

面试解释：

I would keep sparse categorical IDs and dense numeric features separate, because categorical features need embeddings while dense features go directly into the MLP. I would also pass sample weights and label readiness flags because conversion labels are delayed and not every row should contribute to every loss head.

10. Point-in-time Feature Join：如何避免 label leakage？#

Ads ML 最容易被问：offline feature 怎么保证和 online 一致？核心是 point-in-time correctness。

错误做法：

text
training example at 10:00
  joins campaign performance aggregate computed at end of day
  -> leaks future impressions/conversions

正确逻辑：

python
def point_in_time_join(examples, feature_history, feature_key, event_time_col):
    """
    Pseudocode: for each training example, join the latest feature row whose
    feature_timestamp <= event_time. In production this is usually Spark/SQL,
    not a Python loop.
    """
    joined = []
    by_key = {}
    for f in feature_history:
        by_key.setdefault(f[feature_key], []).append(f)

for key in by_key:
        by_key[key].sort(key=lambda x: x["feature_timestamp"])

for ex in examples:
        candidates = by_key.get(ex[feature_key], [])
        event_time = ex[event_time_col]
        latest = None
        for f in candidates:
            if f["feature_timestamp"] <= event_time:
                latest = f
            else:
                break
        joined.append({**ex, **(latest or {})})
    return joined

Production 里会用 Spark window/as-of join：

text
training_examples
  join feature_history
    on entity_id
   and feature_timestamp <= event_time
  qualify row_number() over (
    partition by example_id
    order by feature_timestamp desc
  ) = 1

回答模板：

I would never train ads ranking on features computed after the impression time. I would use point-in-time joins, store feature timestamps, and compare offline feature values against online feature logs to detect training-serving skew.

11. Offline Metrics：AUC / LogLoss / Calibration / NDCG#

模型上线前不能只看 loss。Ads ranking 至少看：

classification：AUC, logloss。 · ranking：NDCG@k, MRR, top-k lift。 · calibration：predicted vs actual。 · business proxy：VCR lift, conversion lift, revenue/yield proxy。

python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score, log_loss

def binary_metrics(y_true, y_prob):
    eps = 1e-6
    y_prob = np.clip(y_prob, eps, 1.0 - eps)
    return {
        "auc": roc_auc_score(y_true, y_prob),
        "logloss": log_loss(y_true, y_prob),
        "avg_pred": float(np.mean(y_prob)),
        "avg_label": float(np.mean(y_true)),
    }

def ndcg_at_k(labels, scores, k=10):
    order = np.argsort(scores)[::-1][:k]
    gains = labels[order]
    discounts = 1.0 / np.log2(np.arange(2, len(gains) + 2))
    dcg = np.sum(gains * discounts)

ideal = np.sort(labels)[::-1][:k]
    idcg = np.sum(ideal * discounts[: len(ideal)])
    return float(dcg / idcg) if idcg > 0 else 0.0

def grouped_ndcg(examples, k=10):
    by_request = {}
    for ex in examples:
        by_request.setdefault(ex["request_id"], []).append(ex)
    scores = []
    for rows in by_request.values():
        labels = np.array([r["label"] for r in rows])
        pred = np.array([r["score"] for r in rows])
        scores.append(ndcg_at_k(labels, pred, k=k))
    return float(np.mean(scores))

面试解释：

AUC tells me if the model separates positives and negatives, but ads serving is a ranking problem. I also need NDCG@k or request-level ranking metrics, plus calibration because auction value depends on probability quality, not only ordering.

12. Calibration：Platt / Isotonic / Temperature Scaling#

广告 auction 会用 bid * pOutcome，所以概率必须 calibrated。

python
from sklearn.isotonic import IsotonicRegression
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression

class PlattCalibrator:
    def __init__(self):
        self.model = LogisticRegression()

def fit(self, logits, labels):
        self.model.fit(np.asarray(logits).reshape(-1, 1), labels)
        return self

def predict(self, logits):
        return self.model.predict_proba(np.asarray(logits).reshape(-1, 1))[:, 1]

class IsotonicCalibrator:
    def __init__(self):
        self.model = IsotonicRegression(out_of_bounds="clip")

def fit(self, scores, labels):
        self.model.fit(scores, labels)
        return self

def predict(self, scores):
        return self.model.predict(scores)

PyTorch temperature scaling：

python
class TemperatureScaler(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.log_temperature = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(()))

def forward(self, logits):
        temperature = torch.exp(self.log_temperature).clamp(min=0.05, max=10.0)
        return logits / temperature

def fit_temperature(logits, labels, steps=200):
    scaler = TemperatureScaler()
    optimizer = torch.optim.LBFGS(scaler.parameters(), lr=0.1, max_iter=steps)

def closure():
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        loss = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(scaler(logits), labels.float())
        loss.backward()
        return loss

optimizer.step(closure)
    return scaler

面试解释：

I would calibrate by segment if needed: device, geo, live/VOD, campaign objective. A model can have good AUC but bad calibration, which is dangerous when the auction multiplies probability by bid.

13. IPS / Doubly Robust：处理 selection bias#

Ads 训练数据有 selection bias：你只观察到被展示广告的 outcome。可以用 exploration logs 里的 propensity。

IPS estimator：

python
def ips_estimate(rewards, action_taken, target_policy_prob, logging_policy_prob):
    """
    rewards: observed outcome for logged action
    action_taken: mask for rows usable by the target policy
    target_policy_prob: probability target policy would choose logged action
    logging_policy_prob: probability logging policy chose logged action
    """
    weight = target_policy_prob / np.clip(logging_policy_prob, 1e-4, None)
    return np.mean(action_taken * weight * rewards)

Doubly robust estimator：

python
def doubly_robust_estimate(reward, q_hat_logged, q_hat_target, rho):
    """
    q_hat_logged: model-estimated reward for logged action
    q_hat_target: model-estimated expected reward under target policy
    rho: target_prob/logging_prob for logged action
    """
    return np.mean(q_hat_target + rho * (reward - q_hat_logged))

面试解释：

I would not trust naive offline conversion lift from selected impressions. I would use exploration traffic, propensity logging, IPS or doubly robust estimators, and then still validate with A/B tests because offline counterfactual estimates have assumptions.

14. Traffic Shaping / Partner Fanout：Python policy sketch#

python
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class PartnerState:
    partner_id: str
    p95_latency_ms: float
    timeout_rate: float
    bid_rate: float
    win_rate: float
    revenue_per_request: float

def should_fanout_to_partner(partner: PartnerState, request_context, latency_budget_ms):
    if partner.p95_latency_ms > latency_budget_ms:
        return False, "partner_too_slow"
    if partner.timeout_rate > 0.05:
        return False, "timeout_rate_high"
    if request_context["is_live"] and partner.p95_latency_ms > 50:
        return False, "live_path_too_risky"
    if partner.bid_rate * partner.win_rate < 0.001:
        return False, "low_expected_value"
    return True, "eligible"

def choose_scoring_path(request_context, candidate_count, model_health):
    if request_context["is_live"] and not model_health["healthy"]:
        return "cohort_or_heuristic"
    if candidate_count > 200 and model_health["p95_ms"] > 30:
        return "two_stage_ranker"
    return "full_model_scoring"

面试解释：

Traffic shaping can be rules plus lightweight models. The point is not to maximize partner fanout blindly, but to decide which demand/model paths are worth the latency and cost under this request's constraints.

15. Offline Replay Simulator：策略上线前怎么评估？#

python
def replay_policy(logged_requests, policy, guardrails):
    metrics = {
        "requests": 0,
        "filled": 0,
        "estimated_revenue": 0.0,
        "cap_violations": 0,
        "latency_budget_violations": 0,
    }

for req in logged_requests:
        decision = policy.select(req["candidates"], req["context"])
        metrics["requests"] += 1
        if decision is not None:
            metrics["filled"] += 1
            metrics["estimated_revenue"] += decision.get("estimated_value", 0.0)
            if decision.get("frequency_cap_exceeded"):
                metrics["cap_violations"] += 1
            if decision.get("estimated_latency_ms", 0) > guardrails["latency_budget_ms"]:
                metrics["latency_budget_violations"] += 1

metrics["fill_rate"] = metrics["filled"] / max(metrics["requests"], 1)
    metrics["revenue_per_request"] = metrics["estimated_revenue"] / max(metrics["requests"], 1)
    return metrics

Limitations 要主动说：

replay 看不到未展示广告真实 outcome。 · partner bids 会随策略变化。 · member behavior 会改变。 · 所以 replay 只能筛掉明显坏策略，不能替代 A/B。

16. Monitoring：PSI / score drift / feature missing#

python
def population_stability_index(expected, actual, bins=10):
    expected = np.asarray(expected)
    actual = np.asarray(actual)
    cuts = np.quantile(expected, np.linspace(0, 1, bins + 1))
    cuts[0] -= 1e-9
    cuts[-1] += 1e-9

expected_counts, _ = np.histogram(expected, bins=cuts)
    actual_counts, _ = np.histogram(actual, bins=cuts)

expected_pct = expected_counts / max(expected_counts.sum(), 1)
    actual_pct = actual_counts / max(actual_counts.sum(), 1)
    expected_pct = np.clip(expected_pct, 1e-6, None)
    actual_pct = np.clip(actual_pct, 1e-6, None)
    return float(np.sum((actual_pct - expected_pct) * np.log(actual_pct / expected_pct)))

def feature_missing_rate(batch, feature_name):
    values = batch[feature_name]
    return float(np.mean(values is None)) if not isinstance(values, np.ndarray) else float(np.mean(np.isnan(values)))

要监控：

score PSI by model head。 · feature missing by feature group。 · categorical unknown-id rate。 · pCTR/pVCR calibration。 · model latency/fallback。 · business guardrails：fill、revenue、delivery、repeat ads、complaints、playback errors。

17. 所有 ML 题的最终回答骨架#

text
1. Define objective and label.
2. Build point-in-time training examples.
3. Split sparse/dense features.
4. Train PyTorch multi-task ranking/outcome model.
5. Evaluate AUC/logloss/NDCG/calibration/business proxy.
6. Calibrate model outputs.
7. Export TorchScript/ONNX.
8. Serve with Triton/Ray Serve/KServe under strict latency budget.
9. Roll out through shadow/canary/A/B/ramp.
10. Monitor feature drift, score drift, model latency, and business guardrails.

Project Stories for Ads Interviews#

1. 项目故事：高吞吐 event pipeline#

讲法：系统需要处理大量客户端/服务端事件，但存在重复、乱序、schema drift。我设计 schema registry、validation、dedup、DLQ、streaming aggregation、quality dashboard。结果是 reporting/billing/decisioning 下游更可靠。这个故事适合 Ads Eventing、Reporting、Measurement、Finance。

2. 项目故事：低延迟 decisioning service#

讲法：原系统请求路径慢且不稳定。我拆分 latency budget，加入 cache、precomputed index、timeouts、fallback、observability。结果 p99 latency 降低，availability 提升。这个故事适合 Ad Server Platform、Decisioning & Optimization、Live Ads。

3. 项目故事：ML model serving 平台#

讲法：模型从离线实验到线上 serving 缺少版本、监控和 fallback。我建立 model registry、feature validation、shadow/canary、score distribution monitoring、rollback。结果模型上线更快更稳。适合 ranking/scoring/optimization 方向。

4. 项目故事：privacy-safe data collaboration#

讲法：业务需要和 partner 协作数据，但不能暴露 raw user data。我设计 clean room/query templates/aggregation thresholds/audit。结果支持 measurement 或 audience use case，同时满足 privacy。适合 Identity、Clean Room、Audience Activation。

5. 项目故事：GenAI creative workflow#

讲法：团队想用 GenAI 提高 creative production，但存在质量、审核、版本、成本问题。我设计 creative lifecycle、variant generation、policy scan、preview/approval、metadata lineage。结果 time-to-approval 降低，creative quality 提升。适合 Creative Infrastructure/Studio。

6. 项目故事：agentic sales/ad ops workflow#

讲法：销售/ad ops 需要从多个系统找信息并手动配置。我设计 agent with tool registry、RAG over entity graph、permission checks、human approval、audit trace。结果减少人工错误和节省时间。适合 Sales Workflows、Platform Foundations、Live Ads Management。

7. Project System Design & Implementation Deep Dives#

下面六个项目不要只背故事，要能画 architecture、讲数据模型、讲 online/offline workflow、讲 failure mode 和 metrics。Ads 面试尤其看重 correctness、latency、business impact、privacy 和 operational explainability。

Project 1：High-throughput ads event pipeline#

Requirements：收集 client beacons、server-side decision logs、SSAI/DAI events、partner callbacks、billing events。系统要支持 high throughput、schema evolution、dedup、late events、privacy filtering、replay/backfill，并为 decisioning、frequency cap、reporting、measurement、billing 提供可信数据。

Architecture：

Event SDK / collector：接收 client/server events，生成 request_id/session_id/event_id。 · Kafka/Kinesis/PubSub：按 event_type / region / campaign shard 分区。 · Schema registry：强制 schema_version、required fields、PII classification。 · Validation/enrichment stream：补 campaign、creative、device、geo、privacy flags。 · Dedup/state store：按 event semantics 去重。 · DLQ/replay service：坏数据隔离，可修复后重放。 · Realtime sinks：frequency cap、pacing、decision feedback。 · Warehouse/lakehouse sinks：reporting、measurement、billing、finance reconciliation。

Implementation details：

event_id 不能只用 timestamp；常用 request_id + ad_id + event_type + playback_position + source。 · impression、quartile、completion、click、conversion 的 dedup window 不同。 · Realtime pipeline 可以 preliminary，billing/reporting 有 finalization window。 · High-cardinality raw events 进入 lake；低基数 health metrics 进入 Prometheus。 · Privacy filtering 要在进入 broad analytics sink 前完成。

Failure modes：schema drift、hot partition、client retry duplicate、late events、partner callback flood、DLQ silently grows。Ops 指标：consumer lag、validation error rate、dedup rate、late event rate、DLQ size、event freshness、billing reconciliation delta。

Project 2：Low-latency ad decisioning service#

Requirements：在 playback latency budget 内返回 ad pod，不能因为 ML/DSP/identity dependency 慢而卡住播放。必须支持 policy、targeting、frequency cap、pacing、auction/ranking、creative readiness、reason logging。

Architecture：

Request gateway：auth、rate limit、request normalization。 · Context builder：member/device/content/session/privacy context。 · Candidate retriever：campaign serving index，避免全量扫描 line items。 · Eligibility filters：flight、geo、device、policy、brand safety、creative readiness。 · State lookups：frequency cap、pacing/budget、delivery goal。 · Scoring/auction：ML score + bid + priority + pacing multiplier。 · Podding optimizer：duration、competitive separation、diversity。 · Decision logger：selected + rejected reasons。

Implementation workflow：

text
request -> context -> candidate retrieval -> hard filters
        -> cap/pacing state -> scoring/auction -> podding
        -> creative resolve -> response + decision log

Latency design：每个 dependency 有 timeout 和 fallback。Identity lookup 超时降级 contextual；ML scoring 超时用 cached score/heuristic；programmatic timeout 丢弃 bid；creative resolver 只返回 ready creative。核心原则：playback continuity beats ad optimality。

Metrics：p50/p95/p99 latency by stage、fill rate、no-fill reason、fallback rate、model timeout、DSP timeout、pod duration mismatch、decision explainability coverage。

Project 3：Ads ML model serving platform#

Requirements：ranking/scoring/optimization model 从 offline experiment 安全进入 online decisioning。要解决 feature freshness、online/offline skew、model versioning、shadow/canary、fallback、score monitoring、business metric regression。

Architecture：

  • Model registry：model version、feature schema、training data snapshot、eval report。
  • Feature store / serving cache：低延迟读取 user/context/campaign features。
  • Model serving：Triton / custom service / JVM/Python service，支持 batch scoring 或 single-request scoring。
  • Experiment controller：shadow、canary、A/B ramp。
  • Monitoring：latency、missing feature、score distribution、business KPI。
  • Fallback scorer：heuristic or previous stable model。

Implementation details：

  • Feature schema hash 绑定 model version；上线时检查 missing/default feature rate。
  • Shadow traffic 记录 score，不影响 ad decision。
  • Canary 按 region/device/campaign cohort 小流量放量。
  • Score distribution drift、feature null spike、latency regression 自动触发 rollback。
  • Offline eval 看 AUC/NDCG/calibration；online 看 delivery、completion、revenue、member experience guardrails。

Pain points：delayed labels、selection bias、feature leakage、feedback loop、serving latency、model explainability。面试里要强调：ML model is part of a constrained decision system, not the whole ad server。

Project 4：Privacy-safe clean room / data collaboration#

Requirements：支持 advertiser/partner 做 audience overlap、conversion measurement、incrementality、activation，但不能暴露 raw user-level data。要有 access control、query templates、aggregation thresholds、audit、retention、delete support。

Architecture：

  • Partner onboarding：contract、identity namespace、allowed use cases。
  • Data ingestion：hashed identifiers、conversion events、audience files，进入 isolated encrypted storage。
  • Identity match service：输出 privacy-safe match keys / segments。
  • Policy engine：检查 query purpose、fields、threshold、join path。
  • Secure compute：运行 approved templates 或 sandboxed query。
  • Result release：aggregation threshold、noise/suppression、export approval。
  • Audit：who queried what, why, result shape, release decision。

Implementation details：

不允许 arbitrary user-level export。 · Query template 比 free-form SQL 更容易做 policy review。 · Small cohort suppression 防止 re-identification。 · 每个 result 带 data_version、privacy_policy_version、aggregation_threshold。 · Partner delete/retention 要传播到 match tables、segments、reports。

Metrics：match rate、suppressed query rate、policy denial reason、query latency、audit completeness、partner onboarding time。

Project 5：GenAI creative workflow for ads#

Requirements：帮助 advertiser 快速生成/变体化 creative，但必须保证 brand safety、rights、format compatibility、approval workflow、serving readiness、cost visibility。

Architecture：

Creative brief parser：解析 product、audience、tone、CTA、format。 · Asset/brand kit store：logo、font、color、legal disclaimer、approved claims。 · Generation service：copy/image/video variant generation。 · Policy & brand validator：敏感内容、claim、logo usage、format rules。 · Review workflow：draft -> review -> approved -> ready。 · Creative distribution：transcode/package/CDN/serving index。

Implementation details：

每个 generated creative 记录 prompt、model version、source assets、brand kit version、policy result。 · Variants 要有 parent creative lineage，方便回滚和效果分析。 · Serving path 只读取 approved + transcoded + CDN_ready creative。 · Agent/LLM 可以建议 copy，但 legal/high-risk claims require human approval。 · A/B test creative variants 时要避免无限 cardinality 伤害 reporting 和 pacing。

Metrics：time-to-first-draft、approval time、reject reason、creative readiness failure、variant performance lift、cost per approved creative。

Project 6：Agentic sales / ad ops workflow#

Requirements：seller/ad ops 希望 agent 查询 campaign、forecast、inventory、reporting、creative status，并生成 proposal 或执行配置检查。系统要防止 hallucination、越权访问、错误修改 campaign。

Architecture：

Ads entity graph：advertiser、order、campaign、line item、creative、inventory、forecast、report。 · RAG layer：检索 contract、policy、historical performance、forecast explanation。 · Tool registry：read tools、validation tools、proposal tools、write tools。 · Permission engine：按 user role、advertiser、campaign、region 控制。 · Workflow state：agent plan、tool calls、approval、final action。 · Audit trace：reason、source citation、tool result、human approval。

Implementation rules：

  • Read-only insights 可自动回答，但必须带 source/citation。
  • Campaign mutation、budget change、targeting change、publish require approval。
  • Agent 使用 structured tools，不直接拼 SQL 或调用内部 API。
  • 每个 tool call 有 timeout、schema validation、idempotency key。
  • Eval 用真实场景：underdelivery diagnosis、proposal generation、creative issue triage、forecast explanation。

Metrics：task completion、human override rate、incorrect answer report、approval latency、tool error rate、time saved、policy violation rate。

Interview close：

Ads agents should accelerate expert workflows, not silently mutate business-critical campaigns. The platform must make every answer grounded, every action permissioned, and every change auditable.

Behavioral / Leadership 答法#

1. 如何回答 “如何平衡 advertiser outcomes 和 member experience？”#

可以说：我会把 member experience 作为 hard guardrail，比如 playback reliability、ad load、frequency cap、creative quality、brand safety；在 guardrail 内优化 advertiser outcome 和 revenue。长期看，糟糕体验会伤害 supply 和 business。技术上用 policy filters、frequency cap、podding rules、latency SLO、quality metrics 和 experiments 来 enforce。

2. 如何回答 “你如何处理跨团队依赖？”#

Ads 系统天然跨 ad serving、clients、eventing、measurement、finance、sales。我的做法是先定义 shared entity model 和 API contract，再明确 owner、SLO、data contract、change management。跨团队项目不能靠会议推进，要靠 contracts、dashboards、runbooks 和 rollout plan。举例时讲你如何把模糊依赖变成可交付边界。

3. 如何回答 “你如何在 0-1 快速建设中保证质量？”#

先找 hard-to-reverse decisions，比如 identity model、event schema、campaign hierarchy、billing ledger、tenant/privacy boundary，这些要谨慎；对 UI/workflow 可以快速实验。用 paved road、automated tests、observability 和 feature flags 让团队快而不乱。不是所有东西一开始都企业级，但核心 invariants 要早定。

4. 如何回答 “你如何和 science/ML 团队合作？”#

我会把模型上线需求产品化：feature contract、training data lineage、offline eval、online metrics、serving latency、fallback、monitoring。Science 团队负责模型质量，platform 团队负责可靠 serving 和 feedback loop。双方共同定义 success：不只是 AUC，而是 delivery、revenue、member experience、latency。

5. 如何回答 “你如何看待 GenAI in ads？”#

GenAI 在 ads 里最大的价值是 scale personalized/modular creative、accelerate sales/ad ops workflows、summarize insights、generate media plans。但风险是 brand safety、creative quality、rights、hallucination、workflow side effects。生产系统要 human-reviewable、policy-checked、versioned、observable。GenAI should speed up humans, not silently mutate campaigns。

Ads 反问问题#

1. 关于 ad serving / live#

  • 当前 ad decisioning 的最大瓶颈是 latency、fill、pacing、frequency cap，还是 policy complexity？
  • Live events 中最担心的 failure mode 是 manifest、creative readiness、decisioning latency，还是 event measurement？
  • DAI path 中 client-side 和 server-side responsibilities 如何划分？
  • 对 playback continuity 和 ad optimality 的 tradeoff 现在怎么做？

2. 关于 optimization / ML#

Ranking/scoring 主要优化哪些 outcome：completion、conversion、brand lift、revenue、reach? · ML model serving 的 fallback 和 canary 机制是什么？ · Forecasting 和 pacing 如何交互？ · Offline simulation 对 online experiment 的可信度如何评估？

3. 关于 data / measurement#

  • Ads eventing 当前最大的 data quality challenge 是 duplicate、late events、schema drift，还是 partner discrepancies？
  • Reporting metrics 是否有统一 semantic layer？
  • Measurement 中 first-party 和 third-party solutions 如何组合？
  • Clean room 的主要 use case 是 measurement、activation，还是 partner collaboration？

4. 关于 privacy / identity#

Identity graph 的 confidence 如何表达给 downstream systems？ · Audience activation 如何平衡 match rate 和 privacy thresholds？ · 对 contextual targeting 的战略重视程度如何？ · Data retention 和 deletion 在 ads event pipelines 中如何处理？

5. 关于 product / org#

Proprietary neo Ads Suite 当前最需要 platform foundations 支持的地方是什么？ · GenAI foundations 主要服务 creative workflow、sales workflow，还是 ad ops automation？ · Ads Entity Service/knowledge graph 的 first use case 是什么？ · 这个岗位 6 个月内怎样才算 high impact？

30/60/90 天 Ads 计划#

1. 30 天：理解 ads lifecycle 和系统边界#

梳理从 sales planning -> campaign booking -> creative approval -> ad serving -> eventing -> reporting/measurement -> billing 的端到端链路。建立 team map、entity map、data flow、critical SLO、top incidents、metric definitions。快速产出可以是 ads lifecycle diagram、decisioning latency breakdown、event quality dashboard gap list。

2. 60 天：补最高杠杆的 reliability/data quality 缺口#

选择一个核心薄弱点，例如 decisioning observability、event dedup/quality、creative readiness、forecast reproducibility、model serving rollback。建立 structured logs、reason codes、data contracts、alerts、runbook、test fixtures。目标是让跨团队 debug 更快，让系统行为更可解释。

3. 90 天：平台化并建立 repeatable patterns#

沉淀 shared patterns：campaign/entity API、serving index update pattern、model deployment pattern、event schema governance、creative workflow state machine、agent tool permission framework。让多个 Ads teams 能复用，而不是每个团队各建一套。高影响力来自 reducing organizational complexity。

最后复习清单#

1. 你要准备的 8 个故事#

准备 high-throughput data pipeline、low-latency backend、ML serving、privacy/security、cost/reliability、cross-team platform、GenAI/agent workflow、business-critical correctness 各一个故事。每个故事用 problem -> constraints -> design -> tradeoffs -> result -> lesson。Ads 面试尤其喜欢 tradeoff 和 correctness。

2. 必须熟的关键词#

ad server, ad decisioning, DAI, SSAI/CSAI, pacing, frequency capping, podding, auction, programmatic, DSP, inventory, line item, campaign, creative, targeting, identity graph, clean room, measurement, attribution, forecasting, simulation, yield optimization, OMS, billing, reconciliation, brand safety, CTV, live events, eventing, GenAI creative, agentic workflow。

3. 最容易加分的一句话#

For neo Ads, I would treat every ad opportunity as a real-time marketplace decision under strict member-experience, policy, and playback constraints. The platform needs to explain why an ad was eligible, why it won, whether it delivered, how it was measured, and how it gets billed.

4. 最容易踩坑的回答#

不要只说“选最高价广告”。广告系统要考虑 guaranteed delivery、frequency cap、member experience、policy、creative readiness、latency 和 long-term advertiser trust。不要只讲 online serving，忽略 eventing、measurement、reporting、billing。不要只讲 ML AUC，要讲 serving latency、feedback bias、business outcomes 和 fallback。

5. 结束面试时可以总结什么？#

可以说：我对 neo Ads 感兴趣，是因为它把 premium streaming、real-time distributed systems、ML optimization、privacy-safe data collaboration 和 GenAI creative workflows 放在同一个增长平台里。我的强项是把复杂业务对象和实时系统边界理清楚，建立可观测、可解释、可扩展的平台，让广告增长同时保护 member experience 和 advertiser trust。
